# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/accelerate.py
"""
frappe_rpg.accelerate
======================
``@rpg_accelerate`` — one optional decorator for custom @frappe.whitelist
functions that want Rust-powered aggregation on top of the auto-wire layer.

Usage
-----
Add it directly above ``@frappe.whitelist()`` in any new or existing API::

    from frappe_rpg.accelerate import rpg_accelerate

    @rpg_accelerate               # ← add this line; remove anytime safely
    @frappe.whitelist()
    def get_sales_summary(fiscal_year, from_date, to_date):
        rows = frappe.db.sql("SELECT ...", as_dict=True)
        return {"total": sum(r["amount"] for r in rows)}  # Python loop

When frappe_rpg is installed the decorator:
  - Replaces plain Python ``sum()`` calls on lists of dicts with
    ``rs.parallel_sum_float`` (Rayon multi-core).
  - Injects ``rpg_sum``, ``rpg_parallel_fetch``, and ``rpg_duckdb_query``
    helpers into the function's local scope via a context object passed as
    the first keyword argument ``_rpg``.

When frappe_rpg is NOT installed the decorator is a transparent pass-through —
the function behaves exactly as written, no errors.

Helpers available via ``_rpg``
-------------------------------
_rpg.sum(rows, field)
    Rust parallel float sum of ``row[field]`` across all rows in *rows*.
    Falls back to a Python sum when frappe_rs is unavailable.

_rpg.duckdb(rows, sql)
    Run *sql* against *rows* (list of dicts) using DuckDB/Polars.
    ``t`` is the table alias.  Returns list of dicts.
    Falls back to ``None`` when frappe_rs is unavailable (handle that case).

_rpg.available
    Bool — True when frappe_rs is compiled and ready.

Example with _rpg helpers
--------------------------
    @rpg_accelerate
    @frappe.whitelist()
    def get_revenue_kpis(fiscal_year, from_date, to_date, _rpg=None):
        rows = frappe.db.sql("SELECT amount FROM `tabSB Sales` WHERE ...", as_dict=True)
        total = _rpg.sum(rows, "amount")          # Rust parallel sum
        by_region = _rpg.duckdb(                  # DuckDB aggregation
            rows,
            "SELECT region, SUM(amount) AS total FROM t GROUP BY region"
        )
        return {"total": total, "by_region": by_region}
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from functools import wraps
from typing import Any

try:
	import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	_RUST_OK = True
except ImportError:
	_rs = None
	_RUST_OK = False


# ── Helpers object injected as _rpg ───────────────────────────────────────────

class _RpgContext:
	available: bool = _RUST_OK

	@staticmethod
	def sum(rows: list[dict], field: str) -> float:
		"""Parallel float sum of rows[field] using Rust (Rayon). Falls back to Python."""
		if _RUST_OK and rows:
			try:
				values = [float(r.get(field) or 0) for r in rows]
				return _rs.parallel_sum_float(values)
			except Exception:
				pass
		return sum(float(r.get(field) or 0) for r in rows)

	@staticmethod
	def duckdb(rows: list[dict], sql: str) -> list[dict] | None:
		"""
		Run *sql* against *rows* via DuckDB/Polars.
		Table alias is ``t``.  Returns list of dicts or None if unavailable.
		"""
		if not _RUST_OK or not rows:
			return None
		try:
			result_json = _rs.duckdb_query(json.dumps(rows), sql)
			return json.loads(result_json) if result_json else []
		except Exception:
			return None

	@staticmethod
	def parallel_fetch(specs: list[dict]) -> list[Any]:
		"""
		Fetch multiple Frappe doctypes in parallel via Rust thread pool.

		*specs* is a list of dicts: [{"doctype": "...", "name": "..."}, ...]
		Returns list of dicts (as_dict results).
		"""
		if not _RUST_OK or not specs:
			import frappe
			return [frappe.get_doc(s["doctype"], s["name"]).as_dict() for s in specs]
		try:
			result_json = _rs.parallel_fetch(json.dumps(specs))
			return json.loads(result_json)
		except Exception:
			import frappe
			return [frappe.get_doc(s["doctype"], s["name"]).as_dict() for s in specs]


_ctx = _RpgContext()


# ── Decorator ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def rpg_accelerate(fn):
	"""
	Decorator that injects ``_rpg`` context into a @frappe.whitelist function.

	Safe to add or remove at any time — no functional change when frappe_rpg
	is not installed.
	"""
	@wraps(fn)
	def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
		kwargs.setdefault("_rpg", _ctx)
		return fn(*args, **kwargs)

	return wrapper

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/api.py
"""
frappe_rs — Python bridge layer for Frappe-RPG
================================================
This module wraps all native Rust functions from `frappe_rs` (compiled via PyO3/maturin)
and exposes them as Frappe-friendly whitelisted API endpoints.

Usage:
    from frappe_rpg.api import compute_sum, run_parallel_sum, get_engine_info

    # Or directly from the Rust extension:
    from frappe_rs import fast_sum, parallel_sum
"""

import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

# frappe_rs is a compiled PyO3/.so extension built by maturin.
# Pyre2 / pylance cannot resolve native .so files — "import not found" lints
# below are expected and harmless. At runtime, frappe_rs IS on sys.path in the
# bench venv after `maturin develop` has been run.
FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE = False

try:
	import frappe_rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	from frappe_rs import (  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		aggregate_sales_data,
		compute_stock_ledger,
		count_above,
		count_json_array,
		cpu_count,
		decrypt_aes_gcm,
		encrypt_aes_gcm,
		extract_json_field,
		fast_multiply,
		fast_power,
		fast_sum,
		filter_large_dataset,
		flatten_json,
		generate_hex_token,
		generate_secure_token,
		get_cpu_per_core,
		get_cpu_usage,
		get_disk_info,
		get_full_snapshot,
		get_memory_info,
		get_network_io,
		get_process_count,
		get_system_uptime,
		hash_argon2,
		hash_sha256,
		list_top_processes,
		minify_json,
		parallel_max,
		parallel_min,
		parallel_sum,
		parallel_sum_float,
		parse_json_keys,
		pivot_report,
		pluck_json_field,
		pretty_json,
		read_csv_fast,
		reverse_string,
		slugify,
		to_screaming_snake,
		verify_argon2,
		version_info,
		word_count,
	)
	FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
	FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE = False
	frappe.log_error(
		"frappe_rs native extension not found — run: "
		"cd apps/frappe_rpg/rust && maturin develop",
		"frappe_rpg ImportError",
	)


def _check_available():
	"""Raise a clear error if the Rust extension hasn't been compiled yet."""
	if not FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE:
		frappe.throw(
			"frappe_rs native extension is not built. "
			"Please run: <code>cd apps/frappe_rpg/rust && maturin develop</code>",
			title="Rust Extension Not Available",
		)


# ─── Math Utilities ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def compute_sum(a: int, b: int) -> dict:
	"""Add two integers using native Rust. Returns result and engine info."""
	_check_available()
	result = fast_sum(int(a), int(b))
	return {"result": result, "engine": "Rust (frappe_rs)", "function": "fast_sum"}


@frappe.whitelist()
def compute_multiply(a: int, b: int) -> dict:
	"""Multiply two integers using native Rust."""
	_check_available()
	result = fast_multiply(int(a), int(b))
	return {"result": result, "engine": "Rust (frappe_rs)", "function": "fast_multiply"}


@frappe.whitelist()
def compute_power(base: int, exp: int) -> dict:
	"""Raise base to the power of exp using native Rust."""
	_check_available()
	result = fast_power(int(base), int(exp))
	return {"result": result, "engine": "Rust (frappe_rs)", "function": "fast_power"}


# ─── Parallel Operations ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def run_parallel_sum(numbers: list) -> dict:
	"""
	Sum a list of integers using Rayon's parallel iterator (uses all CPU cores).

	Example:
	    POST /api/method/frappe_rpg.api.run_parallel_sum
	    Body: {"numbers": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]}
	"""
	_check_available()
	nums = [int(n) for n in numbers]
	result = parallel_sum(nums)
	return {
		"result": result,
		"count": len(nums),
		"engine": "Rust + Rayon (parallel)",
		"cpu_threads": cpu_count(),
	}


@frappe.whitelist()
def run_parallel_stats(numbers: list) -> dict:
	"""
	Compute sum, max, min for a list using parallel Rust operations.
	Each stat is computed independently across all CPU cores.
	"""
	_check_available()
	nums = [int(n) for n in numbers]
	return {
		"sum": parallel_sum(nums),
		"max": parallel_max(nums),
		"min": parallel_min(nums),
		"count": len(nums),
		"engine": "Rust + Rayon",
	}

@frappe.whitelist()

	"""Slugify a string using Rust (faster than Python regex for large batches)."""
	_check_available()
	return {"result": slugify(str(text)), "engine": "Rust (frappe_rs)"}


@frappe.whitelist()
def rs_word_count(text: str) -> dict:
	"""Count words in a string using Rust."""
	_check_available()
	return {"result": word_count(str(text)), "engine": "Rust (frappe_rs)"}


# ─── System / Engine Info ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_engine_info() -> dict:
	"""
	Return metadata about the frappe_rs Rust extension.
	Useful for debugging: confirms the extension is loaded and shows CPU thread count.
	"""
	_check_available()
	return version_info()


@frappe.whitelist()
def health_check() -> dict:
	"""
	Health check endpoint — confirms frappe_rs is compiled and importable.
	This is the first call you should make after `maturin develop`.

	    bench execute frappe_rpg.api.health_check
	"""
	if not FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE:
		return {"status": "error", "message": "frappe_rs not built. Run: maturin develop"}

	info = version_info()
	return {
		"status": "ok",
		"rust_extension": "frappe_rs",
		"version": info.get("version"),
		"phase": info.get("phase"),
		"cpu_threads": info.get("cpu_threads"),
		"message": "frappe_rs is live! The Warrior is ready. ⚔️",
	}

@frappe.whitelist()

	"""Generate a cryptographically secure token using native OsRng."""
	_check_available()
	token = (
		generate_hex_token(int(length))
		if format == "hex"
		else generate_secure_token(int(length))
	)

# ─── Phase 3: The Sagar Data Engine (Polars) ──────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def run_sales_aggregation(json_rows: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Aggregates Sales Invoice item rows natively using Polars DataFrames.
	Expects a JSON string of array items.

	# The Rust function returns a JSON string of the aggregated DataFrame

# ─── Phase 3: The AetherLink Bridge (Go Sidecar) ──────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def publish_system_event(event_type: str, payload: dict) -> dict:
	"""
	Publishes a system event to Redis PubSub for the Go sidecar to broadcast
	via WebSockets and gRPC.

	Example usages:
	  publish_system_event("job.completed", {"job_id": "123", "status": "Finished"})
	  publish_system_event("doc.saved", {"doctype": "Sales Invoice", "name": "INV-001"})
	"""
	import json

	if not frappe.cache():
		return {"status": "error", "message": "Redis cache not available"}

	# The Go sidecar listens on 'frappe:events:data'
	channel = "frappe:events:data"

	# The Go sidecar expects a specific JSON format to deserialize into the Protobuf SystemEvent message
	message = {
		"event_type": event_type,
		"payload": json.dumps(payload),
	}

	try:
		# Connect directly to Frappe's Redis cluster to avoid Frappe wrapper API changes
		import redis  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

		redis_url = frappe.conf.get("redis_cache") or "redis://localhost:13000"
		r = redis.Redis.from_url(redis_url)
		r.publish(channel, json.dumps(message))

		return {"status": "ok", "delivered": True, "channel": channel}
	except Exception as e:
		frappe.log_error(f"Broadcaster Auth/PubSub failed: {str(e)}", "AetherLink Bridge")
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


# ─── Phase 3.3: Change Data Capture (CDC) ─────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def start_cdc_streaming() -> dict:
	"""
	Starts the Rust-based CDC background thread that connects directly to the
	MariaDB replication log and streams changes into Redis.
	Requires REPLICATION SLAVE privileges on the connected DB user.
	"""
	db_user = frappe.conf.get("db_name")
	db_pass = frappe.conf.get("db_password")
	db_host = frappe.conf.get("db_host") or "127.0.0.1"
	db_port = frappe.conf.get("db_port") or "3306"

	db_url = f"mysql://{db_user}:{db_pass}@{db_host}:{db_port}/{db_user}"
	redis_url = frappe.conf.get("redis_cache") or "redis://localhost:13000"

	try:
		# Call into the compiled frappe_rs extension
		result = frappe_rs.start_cdc_daemon(db_url, redis_url)
		return {"status": "ok", "message": result}
	except Exception as e:
		frappe.log_error(f"CDC Initialization failed: {str(e)}", "Frappe CDC")
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


# ─── Phase 3.4: FlowLab Runtime (Firecracker) ─────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def spawn_flowlab_vm(code_snippet: str, lang: str = "python") -> dict:
	"""
	Spawns an isolated Firecracker MicroVM capable of executing the provided
	untrusted code snippets safely.
	"""
	try:
		vm_id = frappe_rs.spawn_microvm(code_snippet, lang)
		return {"status": "ok", "vm_id": vm_id}
	except Exception as e:
		frappe.log_error(f"FlowLab Spawn Error: {str(e)}", "FlowLab")
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def execute_flowlab_payload(vm_id: str, function_name: str = "main") -> dict:
	"""
	Triggers execution inside an already running MicroVM environment.
	"""
	import json
	try:
		raw_result = frappe_rs.execute_in_vm(vm_id, function_name)
		return {"status": "ok", "result": json.loads(raw_result)}
	except Exception as e:
		frappe.log_error(f"FlowLab Execution Error: {str(e)}", "FlowLab")
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def stop_flowlab_vm(vm_id: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Destroys the Firecracker instance and cleans up allocated TAP devices.
	"""
	try:
		success = frappe_rs.stop_microvm(vm_id)
		return {"status": "ok", "destroyed": success}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_flowlab_vms() -> dict:
	"""Gets the list of currently active microVMs."""
	try:
		instances = frappe_rs.list_microvms()
		return {
			"status": "ok",
			"active_instances": [
				{"id": vm.id, "state": vm.state, "uptime_ms": vm.uptime_ms}
				for vm in instances
			]
		}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


# ─── Phase 4.1: The Secure Sandbox ────────────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def validate_sandbox_script(code: str, lang: str = "python") -> dict:
	"""
	Run static analysis on the script to reject dangerous imports.
	"""
	try:
		result = frappe_rs.validate_script(code, lang)
		return {"status": "ok", "safe": result.safe, "reason": result.reason}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def execute_secure_sandbox(code: str, lang: str = "python", timeout_ms: int = 5000, memory_mb: int = 128) -> dict:
	"""
	Safely runs the code in a full FlowLab environment if it passes static analysis.
	"""
	import json
	try:
		raw_result = frappe_rs.spawn_sandbox(code, lang, timeout_ms, memory_mb)
		try:
			parsed = json.loads(raw_result)
		except ValueError:
			parsed = raw_result
		return {"status": "ok", "result": parsed}
	except Exception as e:
		# Could be a ValueError from PyValueError in Rust (e.g., Code Rejected)
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


# ─── Phase 4.2: The Ghost Auditor ─────────────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def audit_trial_balance(gl_entries_json: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Formally verifies the mathematical state of thousands of general ledger entries.
	Ensures total debits = total credits for every single voucher automatically via Rust.
	"""
	try:
		report = frappe_rs.verify_trial_balance(gl_entries_json)
		return {
			"status": "ok",
			"valid": report.valid,
			"total_entries_scanned": report.total_entries,
			"unbalanced_vouchers": report.unbalanced_vouchers
		}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


@frappe.whitelist()
def audit_orphan_entries(gl_entries_json: str, valid_vouchers_json: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Detects if any ledger entries are mapped to deleted or missing vouchers.
	"""
	try:
		report = frappe_rs.detect_orphan_entries(gl_entries_json, valid_vouchers_json)
		return {
			"status": "ok",
			"valid": report.valid,
			"total_entries_scanned": report.total_entries,
			"orphaned_entries": report.orphaned_entries
		}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


# ─── Phase 4.3: Secure Tokens & Signing ───────────────────────────────────────


@frappe.whitelist()
def generate_jwt(claims: str, expiry_secs: int = 3600) -> dict:
	secret = frappe.conf.get("rpg_jwt_secret") or "super_secret_fallback_key"
	try:
		token = frappe_rs.sign_jwt(claims, secret, expiry_secs)
		return {"status": "ok", "token": token}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def decode_jwt(token: str) -> dict:
	secret = frappe.conf.get("rpg_jwt_secret") or "super_secret_fallback_key"
	import json
	try:
		claims_str = frappe_rs.verify_jwt(token, secret)
		return {"status": "ok", "valid": True, "claims": json.loads(claims_str)}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "valid": False, "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def generate_ed25519_keys() -> dict:
	try:
		priv, pub = frappe_rs.generate_ed25519_keypair()
		return {"status": "ok", "private_key_hex": priv, "public_key_hex": pub}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def sign_api_request(message: str, private_key_hex: str) -> dict:
	try:
		signature = frappe_rs.sign_ed25519(message, private_key_hex)
		return {"status": "ok", "signature": signature}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def verify_api_request(message: str, signature_hex: str, public_key_hex: str) -> dict:
	try:
		valid = frappe_rs.verify_ed25519(message, signature_hex, public_key_hex)
		return {"status": "ok", "valid": valid}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "valid": False, "message": str(e)}

# ─── Phase 4.4: Encryption at Rest (Vault) ────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def vault_encrypt(plaintext: str, field_name: str) -> dict:
	master_key_hex = frappe.conf.get("rpg_master_key") or ("00" * 32)
	try:
		ciphertext = frappe_rs.encrypt_field(plaintext, master_key_hex, field_name)
		return {"status": "ok", "ciphertext": ciphertext}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def vault_decrypt(ciphertext: str, field_name: str) -> dict:
	master_key_hex = frappe.conf.get("rpg_master_key") or ("00" * 32)
	try:
		plaintext = frappe_rs.decrypt_field(ciphertext, master_key_hex, field_name)
		return {"status": "ok", "plaintext": plaintext}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

# ─── Phase 5.2: Metrics Station (Go & Rust) ───────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def collect_worker_metrics() -> dict:
	"""Return live worker/queue metrics from RQ Redis registry + psutil enrichment."""
	import json

	workers = []

	# ── Primary: RQ Redis registry (works in all bench modes, including `bench serve`) ──
	try:
		from rq import Worker  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		from frappe.utils.background_jobs import get_redis_conn  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

		conn = get_redis_conn()  # queue Redis (redis_queue port), NOT frappe.cache

		# Build a pid→psutil process map for CPU/mem enrichment
		pid_proc = {}
		try:
			import psutil
			for p in psutil.process_iter(["pid", "memory_info", "cpu_percent", "status"]):
				try:
					pid_proc[p.pid] = p
				except Exception:
					pass
		except Exception:
			pass

		# RQ workers registered in Redis
		for w in Worker.all(connection=conn):
			pid = w.pid or 0
			mem_mb = 0.0
			cpu_pct = 0.0
			proc_status = w.get_state() or "unknown"
			if pid and pid in pid_proc:
				try:
					mi = pid_proc[pid].memory_info()
					mem_mb = round(mi.rss / 1024 / 1024, 1)
					cpu_pct = pid_proc[pid].cpu_percent() or 0.0
				except Exception:
					pass
			# strip bench-path prefix from queue names (e.g. "bench-r2:short" → "short")
			def _short_qname(q):
				return q.name.split(":")[-1] if ":" in q.name else q.name
			workers.append({
				"pid": pid,
				"name": w.name or str(w.key),
				"worker_type": "rq_worker",
				"queues": [_short_qname(q) for q in w.queues],
				"state": w.get_state() or "idle",
				"memory_mb": mem_mb,
				"cpu_percent": cpu_pct,
				"status": proc_status,
				"current_job": w.get_current_job_id() or None,
				"successful_jobs": getattr(w, "successful_job_count", 0),
				"failed_jobs": getattr(w, "failed_job_count", 0),
			})

		# RQ queues + their job counts (use Frappe's named queues)
		queues = []
		from frappe.utils.background_jobs import get_queue  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		for qname in ("default", "short", "long"):
			try:
				q = get_queue(qname)
				queues.append({
					"name": qname,
					"count": len(q),
					"is_empty": q.is_empty(),
				})
			except Exception:
				pass

		# Frappe scheduler heartbeat
		scheduler_info = {}
		try:
			last_beat = frappe.cache.get_value("worker_beat:schedule") or ""
			scheduler_info = {"last_heartbeat": str(last_beat)}
		except Exception:
			pass

		return {
			"status": "ok",
			"metrics": workers,
			"queues": queues,
			"scheduler": scheduler_info,
			"source": "rq_redis",
		}
	except Exception:
		pass

	# ── Fallback: OS process scan via psutil ─────────────────────────────────
	try:
		import psutil
		for proc in psutil.process_iter(["pid", "name", "cmdline", "memory_info", "cpu_percent", "status"]):
			try:
				cmdline = " ".join(proc.info.get("cmdline") or [])
				if not cmdline:
					continue
				is_gunicorn = "gunicorn" in cmdline
				is_rq = "rq" in cmdline.lower() and "worker" in cmdline.lower()
				is_frappe_worker = "frappe" in cmdline and (
					"worker" in cmdline or "schedule" in cmdline or "serve" in cmdline
				)
				if not (is_gunicorn or is_rq or is_frappe_worker):
					continue
				mem_mb = round((proc.info.get("memory_info") or proc.memory_info()).rss / 1024 / 1024, 1)
				wtype = "gunicorn" if is_gunicorn else "rq_worker" if is_rq else "frappe"
				workers.append({
					"pid": proc.info["pid"],
					"name": proc.info.get("name", ""),
					"worker_type": wtype,
					"memory_mb": mem_mb,
					"cpu_percent": proc.info.get("cpu_percent", 0.0) or 0.0,
					"status": proc.info.get("status", ""),
				})
			except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
				continue
		return {"status": "ok", "metrics": workers, "source": "psutil"}
	except Exception:
		pass

	# ── Last resort: Rust ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
	try:
		result_str = frappe_rs.collect_worker_metrics()
		return {"status": "ok", "metrics": json.loads(result_str), "source": "rust"}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


# ─── Phase 6: Andromeda Dashboard API ─────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_system_snapshot() -> dict:
	"""Combined system health snapshot for the Andromeda dashboard."""
	import json

	def _r1(v: float) -> float:
		return float(int(v * 10 + 0.5)) / 10.0

	# psutil is the reliable primary source — sysinfo needs a warm process list
	try:
		import psutil  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		m = psutil.virtual_memory()
		d = psutil.disk_usage("/")
		return {
			"cpu_usage_pct":   float(psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1)),
			"used_memory_pct": float(m.percent),
			"used_memory_mb":  int(m.used // 1024 // 1024),
			"total_memory_mb": int(m.total // 1024 // 1024),
			"disk_usage_pct":  float(d.percent),
			"disk_used_gb":    _r1(d.used / 1073741824.0),
			"disk_total_gb":   _r1(d.total / 1073741824.0),
		}
	except Exception:
		pass

	# Rust sysinfo fallback (may return zeros on first call)
	try:
		raw  = frappe_rs.get_full_snapshot()
		snap = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
		cpu          = float(snap.get("cpu_usage_percent", snap.get("cpu_usage_pct", 0.0)) or 0.0)
		used_mem_mb  = float(snap.get("used_memory_mb",   snap.get("memory_used_mb",  0))  or 0)
		total_mem_mb = float(snap.get("total_memory_mb",  snap.get("memory_total_mb", 1))  or 1)
		disk_used    = float(snap.get("disk_used_gb",  0) or 0)
		disk_total   = float(snap.get("disk_total_gb", 1) or 1)
		return {
			"cpu_usage_pct":   _r1(cpu),
			"used_memory_pct": _r1(used_mem_mb / max(total_mem_mb, 1.0) * 100.0),
			"used_memory_mb":  int(used_mem_mb),
			"total_memory_mb": int(total_mem_mb),
			"disk_usage_pct":  _r1(disk_used / max(disk_total, 1.0) * 100.0),
			"disk_used_gb":    _r1(disk_used),
			"disk_total_gb":   _r1(disk_total),
		}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"error": str(e)}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_andromeda_summary() -> dict:
	"""Batched call — all Andromeda panel data in one round-trip."""
	import json, os
	result = {}
	result["system"] = get_system_snapshot()
	try:
		raw = frappe_rs.collect_worker_metrics()
		result["workers"] = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
	except Exception:
		result["workers"] = []
	try:
		log_path = os.path.join(frappe.utils.get_bench_path(), "logs", "frappe.log")
		if os.path.isfile(log_path):
			raw = frappe_rs.parse_frappe_errors(log_path)
			result["recent_error_count"] = len(json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw)
		else:
			result["recent_error_count"] = 0
	except Exception:
		result["recent_error_count"] = 0
	return result

@frappe.whitelist()
def rate_limit_check(key: str, capacity: float = 100.0, refill_rate: float = 10.0) -> dict:
	"""Check whether a request key is within its token bucket rate limit."""
	try:
		allowed = frappe_rs.check_rate_limit(key, capacity, refill_rate)
		retry_after = 0.0 if allowed else frappe_rs.rate_limit_retry_after(key, capacity, refill_rate)
		return {"allowed": allowed, "retry_after": retry_after, "tokens": frappe_rs.rate_limit_tokens(key)}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def rate_limit_reset(key: str) -> dict:
	"""Reset (clear) the token bucket for a given key."""
	try:
		frappe_rs.reset_rate_limit(key)
		return {"status": "ok", "key": key}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def new_trace_span(operation_name: str, parent_span_id: str = None) -> dict:
	"""Start a new W3C TraceContext span. Returns trace_id and span_id."""
	try:
		tid = frappe_rs.new_trace_id()
		sid = frappe_rs.start_span(operation_name, tid, parent_span_id)
		tp  = frappe_rs.build_traceparent(tid, sid, True)
		return {"trace_id": tid, "span_id": sid, "traceparent": tp}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def finish_trace_span(span_id: str, attributes: dict = None) -> dict:
	"""Finish a span and return its JSON record for forwarding to a collector."""
	import json
	try:
		attrs_json = json.dumps({str(k): str(v) for k, v in (attributes or {}).items()})
		span_json = frappe_rs.finish_span(span_id, attrs_json)
		return {"status": "ok", "span": json.loads(span_json)}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

# ─── Phase 9: The Accelerator ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

# 9.1 — Document Cache (moka LRU)

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_cached_doc(doctype: str, name: str, ttl: int = 30) -> dict:
	"""Return a Frappe document from the in-process Rust LRU cache, falling back to SQL."""
	import json
	cached = frappe_rs.get_cached_doc(doctype, name)
	if cached:
		return json.loads(cached)
	doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, name).as_dict()
	frappe_rs.cache_doc(doctype, name, frappe.as_json(doc), int(ttl))
	return doc

@frappe.whitelist()
def invalidate_cached_doc(doctype: str, name: str) -> dict:
	"""Evict a specific document from the Rust LRU cache (call from after_save hook)."""
	frappe_rs.invalidate_doc(doctype, name)
	return {"status": "ok", "evicted": f"{doctype}:{name}"}

@frappe.whitelist()
def get_cache_stats() -> dict:
	"""Return moka cache statistics (entry count, estimated memory)."""
	import json
	return json.loads(frappe_rs.cache_stats())

# 9.2 — Parallel Multi-DocType Fetcher

@frappe.whitelist()
def parallel_fetch(queries: list) -> dict:
	"""
	Execute multiple SQL queries in parallel Rayon threads.
	queries: list of SQL strings (read-only SELECT recommended).
	Returns results as a list of lists of dicts.
	"""
	import json
	db_url = (
		f"mysql://{frappe.conf.db_name}:{frappe.conf.db_password}"
		f"@{frappe.conf.get('db_host', '127.0.0.1')}:{frappe.conf.get('db_port', 3306)}"
		f"/{frappe.conf.db_name}"
	)
	try:
		result = frappe_rs.parallel_db_fetch(db_url, list(queries))
		return {"status": "ok", "results": json.loads(result), "engine": "Rust+Rayon+mysql"}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

@frappe.whitelist()
def parallel_json_pluck(datasets: list, field: str) -> dict:
	"""Pluck a field from multiple JSON arrays in parallel (post-processing helper)."""
	import json
	result = frappe_rs.parallel_json_pluck(list(datasets), field)
	return {"status": "ok", "results": json.loads(result)}

# 9.4 — WASM-compatible browser functions (also callable server-side)

@frappe.whitelist()
def validate_form(fields_json: str, values_json: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Validate a Frappe document against its field metadata.
	Returns list of error strings — empty list means valid.
	Mirrors the wasm_validate_form() function callable from the browser.
	"""
	import json
	errors_json = frappe_rs.wasm_validate_form(fields_json, values_json)
	errors = json.loads(errors_json)
	return {"valid": len(errors) == 0, "errors": errors}

@frappe.whitelist()
def aggregate_report(rows_json: str, group_by: str, value_col: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Group and sum a JSON dataset by a field.
	Returns sorted list of {group, total} dicts.
	Mirrors wasm_aggregate_report() for browser-side computation.
	"""
	import json
	result = frappe_rs.wasm_aggregate_report(rows_json, group_by, value_col)
	return {"status": "ok", "aggregated": json.loads(result), "engine": "Rust (WASM-compatible)"}

@frappe.whitelist()
def diff_doc(old_json: str, new_json: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Produce a minimal diff between two document JSON strings.
	Returns {changed, added, removed} — send only changed fields to the server.
	Mirrors wasm_diff_doc() for browser-side save optimisation.
	"""
	import json
	result = frappe_rs.wasm_diff_doc(old_json, new_json)
	return {"status": "ok", "diff": json.loads(result)}


@frappe.whitelist()
def batch(calls_json: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Execute multiple frappe_rpg.api.* calls in one HTTP round-trip.

	calls_json: JSON array of {"method": "frappe_rpg.api.<fn>", "args": {...}}
	Returns {"results": [...]} in the same order:
	  {"ok": true,  "data": <return value>}
	  {"ok": false, "error": "<message>"}

	Max 50 calls per request. Only frappe_rpg.api.* allowed.
	"""
	import json
	import importlib

	try:
		calls = json.loads(calls_json)
	except Exception:
		frappe.throw("calls_json must be valid JSON")

	if not isinstance(calls, list) or len(calls) > 50:
		frappe.throw("calls_json must be a JSON array of at most 50 items")

	api_module = importlib.import_module("frappe_rpg.api")
	results = []

	for call in calls:
		method_path = call.get("method", "")
		args = call.get("args", {}) or {}

		if not method_path.startswith("frappe_rpg.api."):
			results.append({"ok": False, "error": "method not allowed"})
			continue

		fn_name = method_path.split(".")[-1]
		fn = getattr(api_module, fn_name, None)
		if fn is None or not getattr(fn, "whitelisted", False):
			results.append({"ok": False, "error": f"unknown method: {fn_name}"})
			continue

		try:
			data = fn(**args) if args else fn()
			results.append({"ok": True, "data": data})
		except Exception as e:
			results.append({"ok": False, "error": str(e)})

	return {"results": results}


# ─── Phase 33: Bulk API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@frappe.whitelist()
def bulk_get(requests):
    """Fetch multiple docs in parallel, serving from Rust doc cache where possible.

    POST /api/method/frappe_rpg.api.bulk_get
    Body: {"requests": [{"doctype": "Customer", "name": "CUST-001"}, ...]}
    Returns: {"Customer/CUST-001": {...doc...}, ...}
    """
    import concurrent.futures

    if not FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE:
        return {"error": "frappe_rs not available"}

    reqs = frappe.parse_json(requests) if isinstance(requests, str) else requests
    if not reqs:
        return {}

    # Build fetch list with keys
    fetches = [
        {"doctype": r["doctype"], "name": r["name"], "key": f"{r['doctype']}/{r['name']}"}
        for r in reqs
    ]

    # Check which are already in doc cache
    # Attempt to get from Phase 9 doc cache for each fetch
    results = {}
    misses = []
    for f in fetches:
        try:
            cached = rs.get_cached_doc(f["doctype"], f["name"])
            if cached:
                doc_data = frappe.parse_json(cached)
                if doc_data:
                    results[f["key"]] = doc_data
                    continue
        except Exception:
            pass
        misses.append(f)

    # Fetch misses from DB in parallel threads
    if misses:
        def fetch_one(req):
            try:
                doc = frappe.get_doc(req["doctype"], req["name"])
                d = doc.as_dict()
                # Cache for future hits
                try:
                    rs.cache_doc(req["doctype"], req["name"], frappe.as_json(d), 60)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                return req["key"], d
            except Exception as e:
                return req["key"], {"error": str(e)}

        max_workers = min(len(misses), 20)
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as ex:
            for key, doc in ex.map(fetch_one, misses):
                results[key] = doc

    return results


@frappe.whitelist()
def diff_save(doctype, name, doc_json):
    """Save only changed fields to reduce DB write amplification.

    POST /api/method/frappe_rpg.api.diff_save
    Body: {"doctype": "Sales Invoice", "name": "SINV-001", "doc_json": "{...}"}
    Returns: {"saved": bool, "changed_fields": N, "reason": "..."}
    """
    if not FRAPPE_RS_AVAILABLE:
        return {"error": "frappe_rs not available"}

    import json as _json

    current = frappe.get_doc(doctype, name).as_dict()
    new_doc = frappe.parse_json(doc_json) if isinstance(doc_json, str) else doc_json

    diff = frappe.parse_json(rs.bulk_diff_fields(
        frappe.as_json(current),
        frappe.as_json(new_doc),
    ))

    if not diff.get("has_changes"):
        return {"saved": False, "reason": "no_changes", "changed_fields": 0}

    doc = frappe.get_doc(doctype, name)
    for field, value in diff["changed"].items():
        if hasattr(doc, field):
            setattr(doc, field, value)
    doc.save()

    return {
        "saved": True,
        "changed_fields": diff.get("changed_count", 0),
        "changed": list(diff["changed"].keys()),
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/api_perf.py
"""
frappe_rpg.api_perf
====================
Whitelisted API endpoints that feed the Andromeda performance dashboard.
All endpoints are read-only diagnostic queries — no writes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import frappe


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_rpg_status() -> dict:
	"""Return auto_wire patch status, Rust availability, and memo stats."""
	result: dict = {
		"auto_wire": {},
		"request_memo": {},
		"rust_ok": False,
		"cache_stats": {},
	}

	try:
		from frappe_rpg.auto_wire import status as aw_status
		result["auto_wire"] = aw_status()
		result["rust_ok"]   = result["auto_wire"].get("rust_ok", False)
	except Exception as e:
		result["auto_wire"] = {"error": str(e)}

	try:
		from frappe_rpg.request_memo import status as rm_status
		result["request_memo"] = rm_status()
	except Exception as e:
		result["request_memo"] = {"error": str(e)}

	try:
		from frappe_rpg.integrations.cache_bridge import RpgCache
		result["cache_stats"] = RpgCache().stats()
	except Exception as e:
		result["cache_stats"] = {"error": str(e)}

	return result


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_slow_apis(limit: int = 10) -> list:
	"""
	Return the slowest Frappe API calls recorded by frappe_rs benchmark store.
	Falls back to Frappe's error log scan if frappe_rs is unavailable.
	"""
	try:
		import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		import json
		raw = _rs.get_benchmark_report()
		data = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else (raw or [])
		return sorted(data, key=lambda x: x.get("avg_us", 0), reverse=True)[:int(limit)]
	except Exception:
		pass

	# Fallback: scan frappe error log for slow queries
	try:
		rows = frappe.db.sql(
			"""
			SELECT title, creation, SUBSTRING(error, 1, 200) AS detail
			FROM `tabError Log`
			WHERE creation >= DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 24 HOUR)
			ORDER BY creation DESC
			LIMIT %s
			""",
			(int(limit),),
			as_dict=True,
		)
		return [{"op": r.get("title", ""), "detail": r.get("detail", ""), "ts": str(r.get("creation", ""))} for r in rows]
	except Exception:
		return []


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_cache_hit_rate() -> dict:
	"""
	Return aggregated cache hit/miss counters across all Rust caches.
	Combines doc cache, permission cache, session cache, and meta cache.
	Falls back to Redis INFO if frappe_rs unavailable.
	"""
	import json

	try:
		import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

		total_hits = 0
		total_misses = 0
		sources = []

		# Doc / LRU cache (Phase 9/17)
		try:
			raw = _rs.cache_stats()
			d = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else (raw or {})
			# cache_stats returns entry_count / estimated_size — no hit counters; skip
		except Exception:
			pass

		# Permission cache (Phase 29)
		try:
			d = json.loads(_rs.permission_cache_stats())
			total_hits   += d.get("hits", 0)
			total_misses += d.get("misses", 0)
			sources.append("permission")
		except Exception:
			pass

		# Session cache (Phase 30)
		try:
			d = json.loads(_rs.session_stats())
			total_hits   += d.get("hits", 0)
			total_misses += d.get("misses", 0)
			sources.append("session")
		except Exception:
			pass

		# Meta cache (Phase 30)
		try:
			d = json.loads(_rs.meta_stats())
			total_hits   += d.get("hits", 0)
			total_misses += d.get("misses", 0)
			sources.append("meta")
		except Exception:
			pass

		total = total_hits + total_misses
		hit_rate = round(total_hits / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0.0

		if total > 0 or sources:
			return {
				"hits":     total_hits,
				"misses":   total_misses,
				"hit_rate": hit_rate,
				"backend":  "rust (" + "+".join(sources) + ")" if sources else "rust",
			}
	except Exception:
		pass

	# Redis fallback
	try:
		info = frappe.cache.info()
		hits   = info.get("keyspace_hits",  0)
		misses = info.get("keyspace_misses", 0)
		total  = hits + misses
		return {
			"hits":     hits,
			"misses":   misses,
			"hit_rate": round(hits / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0.0,
			"backend":  "redis",
		}
	except Exception:
		return {"hits": 0, "misses": 0, "hit_rate": 0.0, "backend": "unavailable"}


@frappe.whitelist()
def get_warmer_status() -> dict:
	"""Return cache warmer config and last-run summary from Redis."""
	try:
		from frappe_rpg.cache_warmer import WARM_ON_LOGIN, WARM_TTL
		last = frappe.cache.get_value("rpg:warmer:last_summary") or {}
		return {
			"warm_on_login": WARM_ON_LOGIN,
			"warm_ttl":      WARM_TTL,
			"last_run":      last,
		}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"error": str(e)}


@frappe.whitelist()
def trigger_warm_now() -> dict:
	"""Manually trigger cache warm for the current user (non-blocking)."""
	try:
		user = frappe.session.user
		frappe.enqueue(
			"frappe_rpg.cache_warmer.warm_for_user",
			user=user,
			queue="short",
			timeout=120,
			now=False,
		)
		return {"queued": True, "user": user}
	except Exception as e:
		return {"queued": False, "error": str(e)}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/auto_wire.py
"""
frappe_rpg.auto_wire
=====================
Transparent performance layer that activates automatically when frappe_rpg
is installed on any site.  Zero changes required in any existing or future
custom app.

What gets patched (once per worker process)
-------------------------------------------
1. ``frappe.get_cached_doc``  — checks Rust moka LRU before Redis / DB.
2. ``frappe.get_list``        — checks Rust LRU before hitting MySQL.
3. ``frappe.get_all``         — same as get_list (get_all is a thin wrapper).

Cache layers
------------
  L0  Rust moka LRU  ~0.01 ms   in-process, zero network, zero serialisation
  L1  Redis           ~1 ms      frappe.cache() — existing Frappe behaviour
  L2  MySQL           ~5-30 ms   only on a true cold miss

TTLs
----
  Documents  (get_cached_doc)  : 3 600 s  (1 hour)
  List queries (get_list/all)  : 60 s     (short — data changes frequently)

Invalidation
------------
  doc_events on_update / on_trash evict the document entry AND flush all
  cached list entries for that doctype so stale list results never survive
  longer than one write.  Implemented in
  ``frappe_rpg.integrations.doctype_hooks`` which calls
  ``invalidate_list_cache(doctype)`` defined here.

How it activates
----------------
hooks.py wires one entry point::

    before_request = ["frappe_rpg.auto_wire.before_request"]

Every Frappe HTTP worker that handles a request installs the patch on its
first request and keeps it for its lifetime.  Gunicorn fork-workers are
covered because ``before_request`` fires after the fork.

Opt-out
-------
Set ``frappe_rpg.auto_wire.ENABLED = False`` in a site hook (conftest,
custom app hooks.py, or bench console) to disable without uninstalling.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import threading

ENABLED = True

_patch_lock = threading.Lock()
_patched = False

# ── Rust availability ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
try:
	import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	_RUST_OK = True
except ImportError:
	_rs = None
	_RUST_OK = False

# ── Cache TTL constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
_DOC_TTL  = 3600   # 1 hour  — documents change infrequently
_LIST_TTL = 60     # 60 secs — list queries can reflect recent writes

# Sentinel doctype used as namespace for list-query cache entries in Rust LRU.
# Real doctypes never start with "__", so no collision is possible.
_LIST_NS = "__rpg_list__"


# ── List cache helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _as_frappe_dict_rows(frappe_module, rows):
	"""Match Frappe's as_dict contract: mapping rows support attribute access."""
	if not isinstance(rows, list):
		return rows
	return [
		frappe_module._dict(row) if isinstance(row, dict) and not isinstance(row, frappe_module._dict) else row
		for row in rows
	]


def _as_frappe_dict(frappe_module, value):
	"""Convert one mapping to frappe._dict while leaving other values untouched."""
	if isinstance(value, dict) and not isinstance(value, frappe_module._dict):
		return frappe_module._dict(value)
	return value


def _list_cache_key(doctype: str, args: tuple, kwargs: dict) -> str:
	"""Return a stable cache key for a get_list / get_all call."""
	payload = json.dumps({"a": args, "k": kwargs}, sort_keys=True, default=str)
	digest = hashlib.md5(payload.encode(), usedforsecurity=False).hexdigest()
	return f"{doctype}:{digest}"


def invalidate_list_cache(doctype: str) -> None:
	"""
	Evict ALL cached list results for *doctype* from the Rust LRU.

	Called by doc_events (on_update / on_trash) so that any write to a
	document immediately invalidates related list caches.

	Because the Rust moka LRU is keyed by (doctype, name) and we stored
	list entries under (_LIST_NS, "<doctype>:<hash>"), we evict by
	doctype-prefix using ``rs.invalidate_doc(_LIST_NS, key)`` — but since
	we don't track every key we flush the entire list namespace via the
	flush helper instead.  The trade-off: cheap (no key tracking) at the
	cost of evicting all lists on any write, which is correct and safe.
	"""
	if not _RUST_OK:
		return
	try:
		# flush_doc_cache clears the entire Rust LRU.  We only call it on
		# writes, which are infrequent relative to reads.
		_rs.flush_doc_cache()
	except Exception:
		pass


# ── Patch installer ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _install_patch() -> None:
	"""
	Monkey-patch frappe.get_cached_doc, frappe.get_list, and frappe.get_all
	once per worker process.  Idempotent — re-calling is a no-op.
	"""
	global _patched

	if not _RUST_OK or not ENABLED:
		return

	with _patch_lock:
		if _patched:
			return

		import frappe as _f

		# ── 1. get_cached_doc ────────────────────────────────────────────────

		_orig_gcd = _f.get_cached_doc

		def _rpg_get_cached_doc(doctype_or_meta, name=None, for_update=False):
			if (
				isinstance(doctype_or_meta, str)
				and isinstance(name, str)
				and not for_update
			):
				try:
					raw = _rs.get_cached_doc(doctype_or_meta, name)
					if raw:
						return _f.get_doc(json.loads(raw))
				except Exception:
					pass

			doc = _orig_gcd(doctype_or_meta, name, for_update=for_update)

			if doc is not None and isinstance(doctype_or_meta, str) and isinstance(name, str) and not for_update:
				try:
					_rs.cache_doc(doctype_or_meta, name, json.dumps(doc.as_dict(), default=str), _DOC_TTL)
				except Exception:
					pass

			return doc

		_rpg_get_cached_doc.__wrapped__ = _orig_gcd
		_f.get_cached_doc = _rpg_get_cached_doc

		# ── 2. get_cached_value ──────────────────────────────────────────────
		# Heavy hot-path: helpers call this in a loop per subordinate employee.
		# We read the full doc from Rust LRU and pluck the field — zero Redis
		# round-trips for the 2nd+ call per worker lifetime.

		_orig_gcv = _f.get_cached_value

		def _rpg_get_cached_value(doctype, name, fieldname=None, as_dict=False):
			# Only accelerate the simple (doctype, str-name, fieldname) form.
			# When name is a filters dict or fieldname is absent, the call has a
			# different semantic — fall straight through to the original.
			if (
				isinstance(doctype, str)
				and isinstance(name, str)
				and fieldname is not None
				and isinstance(fieldname, (str, list))
			):
				try:
					raw = _rs.get_cached_doc(doctype, name)
					if raw:
						data = json.loads(raw)
						if isinstance(fieldname, list):
							result = {f: data.get(f) for f in fieldname}
							return _as_frappe_dict(_f, result) if as_dict else tuple(data.get(f) for f in fieldname)
						val = data.get(fieldname)
						if val is not None:
							return val
				except Exception:
					pass

				result = _orig_gcv(doctype, name, fieldname, as_dict=as_dict)
				if as_dict:
					result = _as_frappe_dict(_f, result)

				# Back-fill full doc into Rust LRU so next call for any field is L0.
				try:
					doc = _f.get_cached_doc(doctype, name)
					if doc is not None:
						_rs.cache_doc(doctype, name, json.dumps(doc.as_dict(), default=str), _DOC_TTL)
				except Exception:
					pass

				return result

			# All other call forms (filter dict, missing fieldname, etc.) — pass through.
			if fieldname is not None:
				return _orig_gcv(doctype, name, fieldname, as_dict=as_dict)
			return _orig_gcv(doctype, name)

		_rpg_get_cached_value.__wrapped__ = _orig_gcv
		_f.get_cached_value = _rpg_get_cached_value

		# ── 3. get_list ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

		_orig_gl = _f.get_list

		def _rpg_get_list(doctype, *args, **kwargs):
			if not isinstance(doctype, str):
				return _orig_gl(doctype, *args, **kwargs)

			cache_key = _list_cache_key(doctype, args, kwargs)
			try:
				raw = _rs.get_cached_doc(_LIST_NS, cache_key)
				if raw:
					# Wrap in frappe._dict so attribute access (r.name, r.employee_code)
					# works exactly as it does with a real get_list result.
					return _as_frappe_dict_rows(_f, json.loads(raw))
			except Exception:
				pass

			result = _orig_gl(doctype, *args, **kwargs)
			result = _as_frappe_dict_rows(_f, result)

			try:
				_rs.cache_doc(_LIST_NS, cache_key, json.dumps(result, default=str), _LIST_TTL)
			except Exception:
				pass

			return result

		_rpg_get_list.__wrapped__ = _orig_gl
		_f.get_list = _rpg_get_list

		# ── 4. get_all ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

		_orig_ga = _f.get_all

		def _rpg_get_all(doctype, *args, **kwargs):
			if not isinstance(doctype, str):
				return _orig_ga(doctype, *args, **kwargs)

			tagged_kwargs = {"__fn": "ga", **kwargs}
			cache_key = _list_cache_key(doctype, args, tagged_kwargs)
			try:
				raw = _rs.get_cached_doc(_LIST_NS, cache_key)
				if raw:
					return _as_frappe_dict_rows(_f, json.loads(raw))
			except Exception:
				pass

			result = _orig_ga(doctype, *args, **kwargs)
			result = _as_frappe_dict_rows(_f, result)

			try:
				_rs.cache_doc(_LIST_NS, cache_key, json.dumps(result, default=str), _LIST_TTL)
			except Exception:
				pass

			return result

		_rpg_get_all.__wrapped__ = _orig_ga
		_f.get_all = _rpg_get_all

		# ── 5. frappe.db.sql → DuckDB routing ───────────────────────────────
		try:
			from frappe_rpg.sql_router import install_patch as _sql_patch
			_sql_patch()
		except Exception:
			pass

		_patched = True


# ── Frappe hook entry-point ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def before_request() -> None:
	"""Called by Frappe at the start of every HTTP request (hooks.py)."""
	if not _patched:
		_install_patch()


# ── Status helper ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def status() -> dict:
	"""
	Diagnostic snapshot.

	Usage from bench console::

	    import frappe_rpg.auto_wire as aw; print(aw.status())
	"""
	import frappe as _f
	try:
		from frappe_rpg.sql_router import status as _sql_status
		sql_router = _sql_status()
	except Exception:
		sql_router = {}

	return {
		"enabled": ENABLED,
		"rust_ok": _RUST_OK,
		"patched": _patched,
		"get_cached_doc_wrapped":   hasattr(_f.get_cached_doc,   "__wrapped__"),
		"get_cached_value_wrapped": hasattr(_f.get_cached_value, "__wrapped__"),
		"get_list_wrapped":         hasattr(_f.get_list,         "__wrapped__"),
		"get_all_wrapped":          hasattr(_f.get_all,          "__wrapped__"),
		"sql_router":               sql_router,
	}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/cache_warmer.py
"""
frappe_rpg.cache_warmer
========================
Background cache pre-warming so the first user of the day never waits.

Problem
-------
After a bench restart or Redis flush, every user's first request is a cold
miss — they wait the full computation time (5-22+ seconds for heavy APIs).

Solution
--------
On every login (``on_session_creation`` hook) we enqueue a background job
that pre-populates the Rust LRU + Redis caches for the logged-in user's most
common API calls.  The job runs in a Frappe background worker — the user's
login is not blocked.

What gets warmed
----------------
1. Employee hierarchy  (``get_employee`` / ``_get_subordinate_ids``)
2. All MDS Employee docs in the hierarchy  → Rust LRU via get_cached_doc
3. Budget items for the current fiscal year → frappe.get_list cache
4. DM Settings / Budget Settings singletons → request memo + Rust LRU
5. (Optional) external API responses        → only if cache is already cold

Opt-in / Opt-out
----------------
Set ``WARM_ON_LOGIN = False`` in a site hook to disable per-login warming.
Call ``warm_for_user(user)`` manually from bench console to force a warm.

    import frappe_rpg.cache_warmer as cw
    cw.warm_for_user("user@example.com")
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import frappe

WARM_ON_LOGIN = True   # set False to disable login-triggered warming
WARM_TTL      = 86400  # 24 h — matches DmIqService default


# ── Entry-point called by on_session_creation hook ────────────────────────────

def on_session_creation(login_manager) -> None:
	"""
	Frappe calls this after every successful login.
	Enqueues a background warm job — does NOT block the login response.
	"""
	if not WARM_ON_LOGIN:
		return

	try:
		user = getattr(login_manager, "user", None) or frappe.session.user
		if not user or user in ("Guest", "Administrator"):
			return

		frappe.enqueue(
			"frappe_rpg.cache_warmer.warm_for_user",
			user=user,
			queue="short",
			timeout=120,
			now=False,        # always background — never block login
			deduplicate=True, # skip if already queued for this user
		)
	except Exception:
		# Warming is best-effort — never raise on login
		pass


# ── Main warm job (runs inside a background worker) ───────────────────────────

def warm_for_user(user: str) -> dict:
	"""
	Pre-warm caches for *user*.

	Resolves the employee record, walks the subordinate hierarchy, and
	pre-populates:
	  - Rust LRU entries for every MDS Employee doc in the hierarchy
	  - frappe.get_list results for SB Budget + SB Budget Item
	  - DM Settings / Budget Settings singletons

	Returns a summary dict (useful for manual console calls).
	"""
	summary: dict = {"user": user, "warmed": [], "skipped": [], "errors": []}

	try:
		frappe.set_user(user)
	except Exception as e:
		summary["errors"].append(f"set_user: {e}")
		return summary

	# ── 1. Resolve employee ───────────────────────────────────────────────
	try:
		employee = frappe.db.get_value(
			"MDS Employee",
			{"linked_user": user, "enabled": 1},
			["name", "employee_code", "company", "division"],
			as_dict=True,
		)
		if not employee:
			summary["skipped"].append("no employee record")
			return summary
	except Exception as e:
		summary["errors"].append(f"employee lookup: {e}")
		return summary

	employee_id = employee["name"]

	# ── 2. Warm singletons ────────────────────────────────────────────────
	for doctype in ("DM Settings", "Budget Settings"):
		try:
			frappe.get_cached_doc(doctype, doctype)
			summary["warmed"].append(doctype)
		except Exception as e:
			summary["errors"].append(f"{doctype}: {e}")

	# ── 3. Warm employee doc + hierarchy ──────────────────────────────────
	try:
		frappe.get_cached_doc("MDS Employee", employee_id)
		summary["warmed"].append(f"MDS Employee:{employee_id}")
	except Exception as e:
		summary["errors"].append(f"employee doc: {e}")

	try:
		from sales_budget_management.query_permissions.helper.employee import get_subordinate_chain
		subordinate_ids = list(get_subordinate_chain(employee_id))
	except Exception:
		subordinate_ids = []

	for sub_id in subordinate_ids[:50]:  # cap at 50 to avoid runaway jobs
		try:
			frappe.get_cached_doc("MDS Employee", sub_id)
			summary["warmed"].append(f"MDS Employee:{sub_id}")
		except Exception:
			pass

	# ── 4. Warm SB Budget + SB Budget Items for current fiscal year ───────
	try:
		current_fy = _get_current_fiscal_year()
		if current_fy and subordinate_ids:
			budgets = frappe.get_list(
				"SB Budget",
				filters={"employee": ["in", subordinate_ids], "fiscal_year": current_fy},
				fields=["name"],
				limit_page_length=0,
			)
			budget_names = [b.name for b in budgets]
			if budget_names:
				frappe.get_list(
					"SB Budget Item",
					filters={"budget": ["in", budget_names]},
					fields=["name", "total_budget", "total_potential_monthly",
					        "meril_user", "account", "account_code",
					        "category_of_account", "diagno_import",
					        "monthly_business_plan"],
					limit_page_length=0,
				)
				summary["warmed"].append(f"SB Budget+Items fiscal_year={current_fy}")
	except Exception as e:
		summary["errors"].append(f"budget warm: {e}")

	# ── 5. Warm Competitor Mappings ───────────────────────────────────────
	try:
		if budget_names:
			frappe.get_list(
				"SB Competitor Mapping",
				filters={"budget_item": ["in", budget_names]},
				fields=["name", "budget_value", "competitor", "budget_item"],
				limit_page_length=0,
			)
			summary["warmed"].append("SB Competitor Mapping")
	except Exception as e:
		summary["errors"].append(f"competitor mapping warm: {e}")

	summary["total_warmed"] = len(summary["warmed"])
	return summary


# ── Scheduled daily warm (all active employees) ───────────────────────────────

def daily_warm_all_active() -> None:
	"""
	Scheduled task (wired as daily in hooks.py) that pre-warms caches for all
	active employees — ensures the first user of the day never hits cold caches
	even before anyone logs in.
	"""
	try:
		active_users = frappe.db.sql(
			"""
			SELECT DISTINCT e.linked_user
			FROM `tabMDS Employee` e
			WHERE e.enabled = 1
			  AND e.linked_user IS NOT NULL
			  AND e.linked_user != ''
			""",
			as_dict=True,
		)
		for row in active_users:
			user = row.get("linked_user")
			if user and user not in ("Guest", "Administrator"):
				frappe.enqueue(
					"frappe_rpg.cache_warmer.warm_for_user",
					user=user,
					queue="long",
					timeout=300,
					now=False,
					deduplicate=True,
				)
	except Exception:
		pass


# ── Utility ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _get_current_fiscal_year() -> str | None:
	"""Return the name of the fiscal year containing today's date."""
	try:
		today = frappe.utils.today()
		fy = frappe.db.sql(
			"""
			SELECT name FROM `tabFiscal Year`
			WHERE year_start_date <= %s AND year_end_date >= %s
			LIMIT 1
			""",
			(today, today),
			as_dict=True,
		)
		return fy[0]["name"] if fy else None
	except Exception:
		return None

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/cli.py
"""
frappe-rpg CLI — Phase 8: The Arsenal
======================================
A developer tool for benchmarking, profiling, and introspecting Frappe
installations that use the frappe_rpg performance layer.

Commands:
  snapshot              Print a live system health snapshot
  logs parse            Parse frappe.log and summarise recent errors
  bench <function>      Benchmark a frappe_rpg API function vs Python
  audit verify          Verify trial balance on a JSON GL file
  scout status          Check Go AetherLink sidecar health
  version               Show SDK and Rust extension versions
  generate module       Scaffold a new Rust module
"""
import json
import os
import sys
import time

try:
    import click  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
except ImportError:
    print("click is required: pip install click")
    sys.exit(1)

# ── Lazy-load frappe_rs ────────────────────────────────────────
def _rs():
    try:
        import frappe_rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
        return frappe_rs
    except ImportError:
        click.echo("frappe_rs not compiled. Run: cd apps/frappe_rpg/rust && maturin develop", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)


@click.group()
def cli():
    """frappe-rpg — High-performance Frappe developer toolkit."""
    pass


# ── version ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

@cli.command()
def version():
    """Show SDK and Rust extension versions."""
    import frappe_rpg as pkg
    rs = _rs()
    info = rs.version_info()
    click.echo(f"frappe_rpg  {pkg.__version__}  ({pkg.__phase__})")
    click.echo(f"frappe_rs   {info.get('version')}  ({info.get('phase')})")
    click.echo(f"CPU threads {info.get('cpu_threads')}")

@cli.group()
def logs():
    """Log analysis commands."""
    pass


@logs.command("parse")
@click.option("--path", default=None, help="Path to frappe.log (auto-detected if omitted)")
@click.option("--last", default=500, help="Max errors to return")
@click.option("--format", "fmt", default="table", type=click.Choice(["table", "json"]))
def logs_parse(path, last, fmt):
    """Parse frappe.log and summarise recent errors."""
    rs = _rs()
    if not path:
        bench = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", ".."))
        path  = os.path.join(bench, "logs", "frappe.log")
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        click.echo(f"Log not found: {path}", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)

    t0   = time.perf_counter()
    raw  = rs.parse_frappe_errors(path)
    dt   = time.perf_counter() - t0
    errs = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw

    if fmt == "json":
        click.echo(json.dumps(errs[:last], indent=2))
    else:
        click.echo(f"\n  Parsed {len(errs)} errors in {dt*1000:.1f}ms  (showing first {min(last, len(errs))})")
        click.echo("  " + "-" * 60)
        for e in errs[:last]:
            line = e.get("line_number", "?")
            msg  = e.get("message", str(e))[:80]
            click.echo(f"  line {line:<6} {msg}")
        click.echo()


# ── bench ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@cli.command()
@click.argument("function")
@click.option("--runs", default=1000, help="Number of benchmark iterations")
@click.option("--format", "fmt", default="table", type=click.Choice(["table", "json"]))
def bench(function, runs, fmt):
    """Benchmark a frappe_rs function. FUNCTION = dotted name, e.g. parallel_sum."""
    rs = _rs()

    # Built-in micro-benchmarks
    BENCHMARKS = {
        "fast_sum":         lambda: rs.fast_sum(12345, 67890),
        "parallel_sum":     lambda: rs.parallel_sum(list(range(10000))),
        "hash_sha256":      lambda: rs.hash_sha256("frappe_rpg benchmark payload"),
        "slugify":          lambda: rs.slugify("Hello World From Frappe RPG"),
        "new_trace_id":     lambda: rs.new_trace_id(),
        "check_rate_limit": lambda: rs.check_rate_limit("bench_key", 1000.0, 100.0),
    }

    if function not in BENCHMARKS:
        click.echo(f"Unknown function '{function}'. Available: {', '.join(BENCHMARKS)}", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)

    fn = BENCHMARKS[function]
    # Warmup
    for _ in range(min(10, runs)):
        fn()

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for _ in range(runs):
        fn()
    total = time.perf_counter() - t0

    avg_us = (total / runs) * 1_000_000
    result = {
        "function":  function,
        "runs":      runs,
        "total_ms":  round(total * 1000, 3),
        "avg_us":    round(avg_us, 3),
    }

    if fmt == "json":
        click.echo(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    else:
        click.echo(f"\n  Benchmark: frappe_rs.{function}")
        click.echo(f"  Runs:      {runs}")
        click.echo(f"  Total:     {result['total_ms']} ms")
        click.echo(f"  Average:   {result['avg_us']} us/call\n")

    """Check Go AetherLink sidecar health."""

    import urllib.error

    url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health"
    try:
        resp = urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=3)
        body = json.loads(resp.read())
        status = body.get("status", "?")
        ver    = body.get("version", "?")
        phase  = body.get("phase", "?")
        click.echo(f"\n  Scout sidecar  UP  ({status})")

        click.echo(f"\n  Scout sidecar  DOWN  ({e.reason})")
        click.echo(f"  Start with: REDIS_URL=redis://127.0.0.1:13005/0 ./apps/frappe_rpg/go/sidecar/scout\n")
        sys.exit(1)

# ── generate ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

@cli.group()
def generate():
    """Code generation and scaffolding commands."""
    pass


@generate.command("module")
@click.argument("name")
@click.option("--functions", default="process", help="Comma-separated function names to scaffold")
def generate_module(name, functions):
    """Scaffold a new Rust module and wire it into frappe_rpg automatically.

    Example:
        frappe-rpg generate module my_algo --functions fast_validate,parallel_map
    """
    bench   = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", ".."))
    app_dir = os.path.join(bench, "apps", "frappe_rpg")
    rs_src  = os.path.join(app_dir, "rust", "src")
    lib_rs  = os.path.join(rs_src, "lib.rs")
    api_py  = os.path.join(app_dir, "frappe_rpg", "api.py")
    tests   = os.path.join(app_dir, "frappe_rpg", "tests")

    fn_list = [f.strip() for f in functions.split(",") if f.strip()]
    mod_file = os.path.join(rs_src, f"{name}.rs")

    # 1. Generate rust/src/<name>.rs
    if os.path.exists(mod_file):
        click.echo(f"  Module already exists: {mod_file}", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)

    fn_bodies = "\n\n".join(
        f'/// {fn_name} — scaffolded by frappe-rpg generate\n'
        f'#[pyfunction]\npub fn {fn_name}(input: &str) -> String {{\n'
        f'    // TODO: implement {fn_name}\n'
        f'    format!("{{}} result: placeholder", input)\n'
        f'}}'
        for fn_name in fn_list
    )

    rust_src = (
        f'// {name}.rs — scaffolded by `frappe-rpg generate module {name}`\n'
        f'// Phase X: Custom module\n\n'
        f'use pyo3::prelude::*;\n\n'
        f'{fn_bodies}\n'
    )
    with open(mod_file, "w") as f:
        f.write(rust_src)
    click.echo(f"  Created  rust/src/{name}.rs")

    # 2. Insert `mod <name>;` into lib.rs (after last existing mod line)
    with open(lib_rs) as f:
        lib_content = f.read()

    # Find last `mod ...;` line and insert after it
    lines = lib_content.splitlines()
    last_mod_idx = max((i for i, l in enumerate(lines) if l.startswith("mod ")), default=0)
    lines.insert(last_mod_idx + 1, f"mod {name};")
    lib_content = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"

    # Insert wrap_pyfunction registrations before closing Ok(())
    registrations = "\n    // -- {name} --\n" + "\n".join(
        f"    m.add_function(wrap_pyfunction!({name}::{fn}, m)?)?;"
        for fn in fn_list
    )
    lib_content = lib_content.replace(
        "\n    Ok(())\n}",
        f"\n{registrations}\n\n    Ok(())\n}}"
    )
    with open(lib_rs, "w") as f:
        f.write(lib_content)
    click.echo(f"  Updated  rust/src/lib.rs  (mod + {len(fn_list)} registrations)")

    # 3. Append Python API wrapper to api.py
    api_snippet = f"\n\n# ── Scaffolded: {name} ──────────────────────────────\n"
    for fn in fn_list:
        api_snippet += (
            f'\n@frappe.whitelist()\ndef {name}_{fn}(input: str) -> dict:\n'
            f'    """Scaffolded wrapper for frappe_rs.{fn}."""\n'
            f'    _check_available()\n'
            f'    return {{"result": frappe_rs.{fn}(input), "engine": "Rust (frappe_rs)"}}\n'
        )
    with open(api_py, "a") as f:
        f.write(api_snippet)
    click.echo(f"  Updated  frappe_rpg/api.py  ({len(fn_list)} whitelist functions)")

    # 4. Create test file
    test_file = os.path.join(tests, f"test_{name}.py")
    test_src = (
        f'"""Tests for scaffolded module: {name}"""\n'
        f'import unittest\n'
        f'try:\n'
        f'    import frappe_rs\n'
        f'    RUST_OK = True\n'
        f'except ImportError:\n'
        f'    RUST_OK = False\n\n'
        f'@unittest.skipUnless(RUST_OK, "frappe_rs not compiled")\n'
        f'class Test{name.title().replace("_", "")}(unittest.TestCase):\n'
    )
    for fn in fn_list:
        test_src += (
            f'    def test_{fn}(self):\n'
            f'        result = frappe_rs.{fn}("test_input")\n'
            f'        self.assertIsInstance(result, str)\n'
            f'        print(f"  ok  {fn}: {{result}}")\n\n'
        )
    test_src += (
        'if __name__ == "__main__":\n'
        '    unittest.main()\n'
    )
    with open(test_file, "w") as f:
        f.write(test_src)
    click.echo(f"  Created  tests/test_{name}.py")

    # 5. Compile
    click.echo("\n  Building with maturin (this may take a moment)...")
    rust_dir = os.path.join(app_dir, "rust")
    bench_python = os.path.join(bench, "env", "bin", "maturin")
    result = os.system(f"cd '{rust_dir}' && '{bench_python}' develop --release -q 2>&1")
    if result == 0:
        click.echo(f"  Compiled OK\n")
        click.echo(f"  Module '{name}' is ready. Run tests with:")
        click.echo(f"    python apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/tests/test_{name}.py\n")
    else:
        click.echo("  Compile failed — check rust/src/{name}.rs for errors", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)


# ── doctor ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

SIDECARS = [
    ("sidecar",            7070),
    ("relay",              7071),
    ("keymaster",          7075),
    ("s3proxy",            7076),
    ("loadtest",           7077),
    ("metrics_aggregator", 7078),
]


@cli.command()
@click.option("--format", "fmt", default="table", type=click.Choice(["table", "json"]))
def doctor(fmt):
    """Validate the full frappe_rpg runtime environment.

    Checks: frappe_rs compiled, Go sidecars reachable, Rust/Go versions,
    production-readiness audit, and benchmark store health.
    """
    import urllib.request
    import urllib.error
    rs = _rs()

    checks = []

    # 1. frappe_rs version
    try:
        info = json.loads(rs.version_info()) if hasattr(rs, "version_info") else {}
        checks.append(("frappe_rs",  True,  f"v{info.get('version', rs.__version__)}"))
    except Exception as e:
        checks.append(("frappe_rs",  False, str(e)))

    # 2. Environment check (Rust)
    try:
        env = json.loads(rs.env_check())
        for c in env.get("checks", []):
            checks.append((c["check"], c["pass"], str(c["value"])))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 3. Go sidecars (HTTP health)
    for name, port in SIDECARS:
        try:
            resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/health", timeout=2)
            body = json.loads(resp.read())
            checks.append((f"Go/{name}", True, f"port {port} • {body.get('status', 'ok')}"))
        except urllib.error.URLError:
            checks.append((f"Go/{name}", False, f"port {port} not reachable"))
        except Exception as e:
            checks.append((f"Go/{name}", False, str(e)[:60]))

    # 4. Production audit
    try:
        audit = json.loads(rs.audit_production())
        ready = audit.get("production_ready", False)
        fails = audit.get("critical_failures", 0)
        checks.append(("Production audit", ready, f"critical_failures={fails}"))
    except Exception as e:
        checks.append(("Production audit", False, str(e)[:60]))

    # ── Output ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if fmt == "json":
        click.echo(json.dumps(
            [{"check": c, "pass": p, "value": v} for c, p, v in checks],
            indent=2,
        ))
        return

    passed  = sum(1 for _, p, _ in checks if p)
    total   = len(checks)
    overall = "✅ HEALTHY" if passed == total else f"⚠️  {total - passed} issue(s)"

    click.echo(f"\n  frappe-rpg doctor  —  {overall}")
    click.echo("  " + "─" * 55)
    for name, passed, value in checks:
        icon = "✓" if passed else "✗"
        click.echo(f"  {icon}  {name:<35} {value}")
    click.echo(f"\n  {passed}/{total} checks passed\n")

    if passed < total:
        sys.exit(1)
