@cli.group("api-docs")
def api_docs():
    """API documentation commands (auto-generated from frappe_rs.pyi)."""
    pass


@api_docs.command("generate")
@click.option("--output", "-o", default=None, help="Output file (default: stdout)")
@click.option("--format", "fmt", default="markdown", type=click.Choice(["markdown", "index"]))
def api_docs_generate(output, fmt):
    """Generate API reference docs from frappe_rs.pyi."""
    rs = _rs()

    app_dir = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", ".."))
    pyi_path = os.path.join(app_dir, "rust", "frappe_rs.pyi")
    if not os.path.exists(pyi_path):
        click.echo(f"frappe_rs.pyi not found at {pyi_path}", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)

    with open(pyi_path) as f:
        pyi_content = f.read()

    if fmt == "markdown":
        result = json.loads(rs.generate_api_docs(pyi_content))
        content = result["markdown"]
        click.echo(f"  Generated {result['function_count']} functions across {result['section_count']} sections",
                   err=True)
    else:
        result  = json.loads(rs.api_doc_index(pyi_content))
        content = result["table"]
        click.echo(f"  Generated index with {result['row_count']} rows", err=True)

    if output:
        with open(output, "w") as f:
            f.write(content)
        click.echo(f"  Written to {output}", err=True)
    else:
        click.echo(content)


@api_docs.command("diff")
@click.argument("old_pyi")
@click.argument("new_pyi")
def api_docs_diff(old_pyi, new_pyi):
    """Diff two .pyi files and show added/removed functions."""
    rs = _rs()

    for path in (old_pyi, new_pyi):
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            click.echo(f"File not found: {path}", err=True)
            sys.exit(1)

    with open(old_pyi) as f: old = f.read()
    with open(new_pyi) as f: new = f.read()

    result = json.loads(rs.diff_api_manifest(old, new))

    click.echo(f"\n  API diff: {old_pyi} → {new_pyi}")
    click.echo("  " + "─" * 40)
    for fn in result.get("added", []):
        click.echo(f"  + {fn}")
    for fn in result.get("removed", []):
        click.echo(f"  - {fn}")
    click.echo(f"\n  +{result['added_count']} added  -{result['removed_count']} removed"
               f"  {result['unchanged_count']} unchanged")
    if result.get("breaking_change"):
        click.echo("  ⚠️  BREAKING CHANGE: functions removed\n")
    click.echo()


# ── deps ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@cli.command()
@click.option("--format", "fmt", default="table", type=click.Choice(["table", "json"]))
def deps(fmt):
    """Show Rust dependency report (outdated versions, phase groupings)."""
    rs = _rs()

    app_dir    = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", ".."))
    cargo_path = os.path.join(app_dir, "rust", "Cargo.toml")
    if not os.path.exists(cargo_path):
        click.echo("Cargo.toml not found", err=True)
        sys.exit(1)

    with open(cargo_path) as f:
        content = f.read()

    result = json.loads(rs.dependency_report(content))

    if fmt == "json":
        click.echo(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
        return

    click.echo(f"\n  Rust Dependencies  ({result['total']} total, {result['outdated']} possibly outdated)")
    click.echo("  " + "─" * 55)
    for dep in result["dependencies"]:
        name     = dep["name"]
        version  = dep["version"][:20]
        outdated = dep.get("outdated") or ""
        flag     = f"  ← {outdated}" if outdated else ""
        devmark  = " [dev]" if dep.get("dev") else ""
        click.echo(f"  {name:<30} {version:<20}{devmark}{flag}")
    click.echo()

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/doctype/rpg_benchmark/rpg_benchmark.py
import frappe
from frappe.model.document import Document


class RPGBenchmark(Document):
    pass


@frappe.whitelist()
def record_benchmark(function_name: str, duration_ms: float, runs: int,
                     avg_us: float = 0.0, module: str = "frappe_rs",
                     input_size: int = 0, notes: str = "") -> dict:
    """Create an RPG Benchmark record from frappe-rpg bench output."""
    import sys
    try:
        import frappe_rs
        rust_ver = getattr(frappe_rs, "__version__", "unknown")
    except ImportError:
        rust_ver = "unknown"

    doc = frappe.new_doc("RPG Benchmark")
    doc.function_name  = function_name
    doc.module         = module
    doc.duration_ms    = duration_ms
    doc.avg_us         = avg_us
    doc.runs           = runs
    doc.input_size     = input_size
    doc.notes          = notes
    doc.python_version = sys.version.split()[0]
    doc.rust_version   = rust_ver
    doc.site           = frappe.local.site
    doc.insert(ignore_permissions=True)
    return {"name": doc.name, "status": "saved"}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/__init__.py
"""Phase 44 — The FastAPI Lane.

Async ASGI sidecar at :7094 for SSE / fan-out / Pydantic-validated endpoints.
Runs alongside Frappe's Werkzeug WSGI — does not replace it.
"""

__version__ = "0.44.0"

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/app.py
"""FastAPI lane app instance.

Run with: `uvicorn frappe_rpg.fastapi_lane.app:app --port 7094 --loop uvloop`
"""

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from . import __version__
from .errors import http_exception_handler, unhandled_exception_handler
from .middleware import ScorekeeperMiddleware
from .settings import settings

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
    _RS_VERSION = _rs.__version__
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
    _rs = None
    _RS_VERSION = "unavailable"


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):
    yield


app = FastAPI(
    title="Frappe-RPG FastAPI Lane",
    version=__version__,
    description="Async ASGI lane (Phase 44) — SSE / fan-out / Pydantic write surface",
    docs_url="/fapi/docs",
    openapi_url="/fapi/openapi.json",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=list(settings.cors_origins) if settings.cors_origins else ["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)
app.add_middleware(ScorekeeperMiddleware)

app.add_exception_handler(HTTPException, http_exception_handler)
app.add_exception_handler(Exception, unhandled_exception_handler)


@app.get("/fapi/health")
async def health():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "phase": 44,
        "lane_version": __version__,
        "frappe_rs": _RS_VERSION,
        "port": settings.port,
    }


# ─── Wire endpoint routers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

from .endpoints import stream_scorekeeper, bulk_external_lookup, validate_invoice  # noqa: E402

app.include_router(stream_scorekeeper.router, prefix="/fapi", tags=["streaming"])
app.include_router(bulk_external_lookup.router, prefix="/fapi", tags=["fan-out"])
app.include_router(validate_invoice.router, prefix="/fapi", tags=["validation"])

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/auth.py
"""Auth dependency for the FastAPI lane.

Validates Frappe session cookies or API key/secret headers. Does NOT load
Frappe's permission stack — endpoints that need doc-level permission checks
must call `frappe_rs.permission_check(...)` directly.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from fastapi import Header, HTTPException, Request

from .settings import settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class User:
    name: str
    roles: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    auth_kind: str = "anon"


async def current_user(
    request: Request,
    x_api_key: str | None = Header(default=None),
    x_api_secret: str | None = Header(default=None),
) -> User:
    if not settings.auth_required:
        return User(name="anon@local", roles=("Guest",), auth_kind="disabled")

    # API key/secret
    if x_api_key and x_api_secret:
        # Real impl: validate against `tabUser` API key store (or call Frappe).
        # For the lane we trust matched secret tokens that are 32+ hex chars.
        if len(x_api_secret) >= 32:
            return User(name=f"apikey:{x_api_key[:8]}", roles=("System User",), auth_kind="api_key")
        raise HTTPException(401, "invalid api_secret")

    # Frappe session cookie (`sid`)
    sid = request.cookies.get("sid")
    if sid and len(sid) >= 20:
        # Real impl: lookup `sid` in Redis session store (`session:{sid}`).
        # Tests use this path with a synthetic >=20-char cookie.
        return User(name=f"sid:{sid[:8]}", roles=("System User",), auth_kind="session")

    raise HTTPException(401, "authentication required")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/endpoints/bulk_external_lookup.py
"""Async fan-out for external party-validation lookups (GSTIN / MCA / IEC).

Demonstrates the FastAPI lane's primary win: N external HTTP calls in parallel
via `httpx.AsyncClient` + `asyncio.gather`. Each call is short-circuited if the
GSTIN fails Phase 37's MOD-36 validator — no point spending an external call on
a syntactically invalid GSTIN.
"""

import asyncio
import json
from typing import Literal

import httpx
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from ..auth import User, current_user
from ..settings import settings

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
    _rs = None

router = APIRouter()


class LookupRequest(BaseModel):
    gstins: list[str] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100)
    sources: list[Literal["gst_portal", "mca", "iec"]] = Field(default=["gst_portal"])
    timeout_s: float = Field(default=8.0, gt=0, le=30)


_SOURCE_URLS = {
    # Stubs — replace with real endpoints in production. The lane just needs
    # to demonstrate the fan-out pattern.
    "gst_portal": "https://stub.example/gstin/lookup",
    "mca": "https://stub.example/mca/cin",
    "iec": "https://stub.example/iec/lookup",
}


async def _call_one(client: httpx.AsyncClient, source: str, gstin: str):
    try:
        r = await client.post(_SOURCE_URLS[source], json={"gstin": gstin})
        return {"source": source, "gstin": gstin, "status": r.status_code,
                "data": r.json() if r.headers.get("content-type", "").startswith("application/json") else r.text[:500]}
    except httpx.HTTPError as e:
        return {"source": source, "gstin": gstin, "status": 0, "error": type(e).__name__}


@router.post("/bulk/external_lookup")
async def bulk_external_lookup(req: LookupRequest, user: User = Depends(current_user)):
    if _rs is None:
        raise HTTPException(503, "frappe_rs not available")

    # 1. Pre-validate GSTINs via Rust (free, no external calls)
    pre = json.loads(_rs.tax_validate_gstin_bulk(json.dumps(req.gstins)))
    valid_gstins = pre["valid"]
    invalid = pre["invalid"]

    if not valid_gstins:
        return {"valid": 0, "invalid": invalid, "results": []}

    # 2. Fan out only the valid ones
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=req.timeout_s) as client:
        tasks = [
            _call_one(client, src, g)
            for g in valid_gstins
            for src in req.sources
        ]
        results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=False)

    return {
        "valid": len(valid_gstins),
        "invalid": invalid,
        "results": results,
        "user": user.name,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/endpoints/stream_scorekeeper.py
"""SSE live feed from the Phase 43 Scorekeeper ring buffer."""

import asyncio
import json

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request
from sse_starlette.sse import EventSourceResponse

from ..auth import User, current_user

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
    _rs = None

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/stream/scorekeeper")
async def scorekeeper_stream(
    request: Request,
    interval: float = 1.0,
    user: User = Depends(current_user),
):
    """Emit `stats` SSE events every `interval` seconds until the client disconnects."""

    async def event_gen():
        try:
            while True:
                if await request.is_disconnected():
                    break
                if _rs is None:
                    yield {"event": "error", "data": json.dumps({"error": "frappe_rs not loaded"})}
                    return
                payload = json.loads(_rs.sk_get_all_stats())
                summary = json.loads(_rs.sk_get_savings_today())
                yield {
                    "event": "stats",
                    "data": json.dumps({
                        "user": user.name,
                        "endpoints": payload,
                        "savings_today": summary,
                    }),
                }
                await asyncio.sleep(max(0.05, interval))
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            return

    return EventSourceResponse(event_gen())

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/endpoints/validate_invoice.py
"""Pydantic v2 + Phase 37 Rust tax engine for invoice validation."""

import json
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, StringConstraints

from ..auth import User, current_user

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
    _rs = None

router = APIRouter()


GstinStr = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(
        pattern=r"^\d{2}[A-Z]{5}\d{4}[A-Z][1-9A-Z]Z[0-9A-Z]$",
        min_length=15, max_length=15,
    ),
]


class InvoiceLine(BaseModel):
    item_code: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=140)
    qty: Decimal = Field(..., gt=0)
    rate: Decimal = Field(..., ge=0)
    discount_pct: Decimal = Field(default=Decimal("0"), ge=0, le=100)
    gst_rate: Decimal = Field(..., ge=0, le=28)

    @property
    def taxable(self) -> Decimal:
        gross = self.qty * self.rate
        return gross * (Decimal("100") - self.discount_pct) / Decimal("100")


class InvoicePayload(BaseModel):
    customer: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=140)
    posting_date: date
    supplier_gstin: GstinStr
    buyer_gstin: GstinStr
    lines: list[InvoiceLine] = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=500)


@router.post("/validate/invoice")
async def validate_invoice(payload: InvoicePayload, user: User = Depends(current_user)):
    if _rs is None:
        raise HTTPException(503, "frappe_rs not available")

    invoice_for_rust = {
        "supplier_gstin": payload.supplier_gstin,
        "buyer_gstin": payload.buyer_gstin,
        "lines": [
            {"taxable": str(line.taxable), "rate": str(line.gst_rate)}
            for line in payload.lines
        ],
    }
    summary = json.loads(_rs.tax_invoice_summary(json.dumps(invoice_for_rust)))
    return {
        "customer": payload.customer,
        "posting_date": payload.posting_date.isoformat(),
        "line_count": len(payload.lines),
        "user": user.name,
        **summary,
    }

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/errors.py
"""JSON exception handlers — never leak Werkzeug-style HTML errors."""

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse


async def http_exception_handler(_: Request, exc: HTTPException):
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
        content={"error": exc.detail, "status": exc.status_code},
    )


async def unhandled_exception_handler(_: Request, exc: Exception):
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=500,
        content={"error": "internal_error", "type": type(exc).__name__, "msg": str(exc)[:200]},
    )

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/middleware.py
"""Per-request middleware: Scorekeeper timing + layer attribution."""

import time
import uuid

from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
from starlette.requests import Request

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
    _SK_AVAILABLE = True
except Exception:  # pragma: no cover
    _rs = None
    _SK_AVAILABLE = False


class ScorekeeperMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    """Records every request into the Phase 43 ring buffer."""

    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next):
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        request.state.request_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        request.state.layer = "L0"  # FastAPI lane default — Python+Rust in-process

        try:
            response = await call_next(request)
        except Exception:
            elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
            if _SK_AVAILABLE:
                _rs.sk_record(f"fapi::{request.url.path}", "L4", elapsed, 0.0)
            raise

        elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
        endpoint = f"fapi::{request.url.path}"
        if _SK_AVAILABLE:
            _rs.sk_record(endpoint, request.state.layer, elapsed_ms, 0.0)

        response.headers["X-RPG-Cache"] = request.state.layer
        response.headers["X-RPG-Time-Ms"] = str(round(elapsed_ms, 2))
        response.headers["X-RPG-Request-Id"] = request.state.request_id
        return response

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/fastapi_lane/settings.py
"""FastAPI lane runtime config — env-driven, no Frappe required."""

import os

class Settings:
    port: int = int(os.environ.get("FASTAPI_LANE_PORT", "7094"))
    multiplexer_url: str = os.environ.get("DB_MULTIPLEXER_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:7086")
    vanguard_url: str = os.environ.get("VANGUARD_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:7092")
    notify_url: str = os.environ.get("NOTIFY_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:7090")
    redis_url: str = os.environ.get("REDIS_URL", "redis://127.0.0.1:6379/0")
    request_timeout_s: float = float(os.environ.get("FASTAPI_LANE_TIMEOUT", "10"))
    auth_required: bool = os.environ.get("FASTAPI_LANE_AUTH", "1") != "0"
    cors_origins: tuple = tuple(
        o.strip() for o in os.environ.get("FASTAPI_LANE_CORS", "*").split(",") if o.strip()
    )


settings = Settings()

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AndromedaDash controller
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

class AndromedaDash {
  constructor() {
    this._chart = null;
    this._errorHistory = new Array(60).fill(0); // last 60 minutes
    this._ws = null;
    this._wsReconnectTimer = null;
    this._refreshTimer = null;
    this._METRICS_PORT = frappe.boot?.rpg_metrics_port || 7070;
  }

  start() {
    this.refresh();
    this._refreshTimer = setInterval(() => this.refresh(), 15_000); // auto every 15s
    this._connectWs();
    this._pollErrors();
    setInterval(() => this._pollErrors(), 30_000);
  }

  // ── Main refresh ──────────────────────────────────────────
  async refresh() {
    this._loadSystemHealth();
    this._loadWorkers();
    this._loadQueues();
    this._loadSecurityAudit();
    this._loadRpgStatus();
    this._loadRpgCacheHitRate();
    this._loadRpgWarmer();
    this._loadRpgSlowApis();
  }

  // ── 6.1 System Health Panel ───────────────────────────────
  async _loadSystemHealth() {
    try {
      const data = await frappe.call({
        method: "frappe_rpg.api.get_system_snapshot",
      });
      const s = data.message || {};
      this._renderStatCard("and-cpu-card",    "CPU Usage",  s.cpu_usage_pct, "%", "🖥");
      this._renderStatCard("and-mem-card",    "Memory",     s.used_memory_pct, "%", "🧠",
        `${s.used_memory_mb || 0} MB / ${s.total_memory_mb || 0} MB`);
      this._renderStatCard("and-disk-card",   "Disk Usage", s.disk_usage_pct, "%", "💾",
        `${s.disk_used_gb || 0} GB / ${s.disk_total_gb || 0} GB`);
    } catch (_) {
      this._renderError("and-cpu-card", "Metrics unavailable");
    }
  }

  _renderStatCard(id, title, value, unit, icon, sublabel) {
    const pct  = Math.min(parseFloat(value) || 0, 100);
    const cls  = pct > 85 ? "red" : pct > 65 ? "yellow" : "green";
    const col  = pct > 85 ? "var(--and-red)" : pct > 65 ? "var(--and-yellow)" : "var(--and-green)";
    $("#" + id).html(`
      <div class="and-card-header">
        <span class="and-card-title">${title}</span>
        <span class="and-card-icon">${icon}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="and-stat" style="color:${col}">${pct.toFixed(1)}<span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:400">${unit}</span></div>
      ${sublabel ? `<div class="and-stat-label">${sublabel}</div>` : ""}
      <div class="and-gauge-wrap">
        <div class="and-gauge-track">
          <div class="and-gauge-fill ${cls}" style="width:${pct}%"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `);
  }

  // ── 6.2 Error Rate Chart ──────────────────────────────────
  async _pollErrors() {
    try {
      const bench_path = frappe.boot?.bench_path || "/home/frappe/frappe-bench";
      const data = await frappe.call({
        method: "frappe_rpg.api.scan_frappe_errors",
        args: { log_path: bench_path + "/logs/frappe.log" },
      });
      const count = (data.message || []).length;
      this._errorHistory.push(count);
      this._errorHistory.shift();
      this._renderErrorChart();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  _renderErrorChart() {
    const max   = Math.max(...this._errorHistory, 1);
    const labels = this._errorHistory.map((_, i) => i === 59 ? "now" : i === 30 ? "-30m" : "");
    const colors = this._errorHistory.map(v =>
      v > max * 0.7 ? "#da3633cc" : v > max * 0.4 ? "#d29922cc" : "#3fb950cc"
    );

    if (this._chart) {
      this._chart.data.datasets[0].data   = [...this._errorHistory];
      this._chart.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = colors;
      this._chart.update("none");
      return;
    }

    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.style.height = "110px";
    $("#and-error-chart-wrap").html("").append(canvas);

    this._chart = new Chart(canvas, {
      type: "bar",
      data: {
        labels,
        datasets: [{
          data: [...this._errorHistory],
          backgroundColor: colors,
          borderRadius: 2,
          borderSkipped: false,
        }],
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: {
          callbacks: { label: ctx => `${ctx.raw} errors` },
        }},
        scales: {
          x: { grid: { color: "#21262d" }, ticks: { color: "#8b949e", font: { size: 9 } } },
          y: { grid: { color: "#21262d" }, ticks: { color: "#8b949e", font: { size: 9 } }, min: 0 },
        },
      },
    });
  }

  // ── 6.3 Live Event Feed (WebSocket) ───────────────────────
  _connectWs() {
    if (this._ws && this._ws.readyState < 2) return;
    const wsUrl = `ws://${location.hostname}:7072/ws`;
    try {
      this._ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
      this._ws.onmessage = (evt) => this._onWsMessage(evt);
      this._ws.onclose   = () => {
        this._wsReconnectTimer = setTimeout(() => this._connectWs(), 5000);
      };
      this._ws.onerror = () => { this._ws.close(); };
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  _onWsMessage(evt) {
    try {
      const msg = JSON.parse(evt.data);
      const type = (msg.type || "").toLowerCase();
      const cls  = type.includes("security") ? "security"
                 : type.includes("error")    ? "warning"
                 : type.includes("job")      ? "job"
                 : "normal";
      const ts  = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
      const row = `
        <div class="and-event">
          <span class="and-event-time">${ts}</span>
          <span class="and-event-type-${cls}">${msg.type || "event"}</span>
          <span style="color:var(--and-text);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">
            ${frappe.utils.escape_html(JSON.stringify(msg.payload || msg).slice(0, 100))}
          </span>
        </div>`;
      const feed = $("#and-event-feed");
      feed.prepend(row);
      if (feed.children().length > 80) feed.children(":gt(79)").remove();
      // Auto-pause scroll if user is reading
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ── 6.4 Worker Processes ──────────────────────────────────
  async _loadWorkers() {
    try {
      const data = await frappe.call({ method: "frappe_rpg.api.collect_worker_metrics" });
      const msg = data.message || {};
      // API returns {status, metrics:[...], queues:[...], source} or legacy flat array
      const workers = Array.isArray(msg) ? msg : (msg.metrics || []);
      const queues  = Array.isArray(msg) ? [] : (msg.queues  || []);

      if (!workers.length && !queues.length) {
        $("#and-workers-grid").html(`<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">No workers found</span>`);
        return;
      }

      const workerTiles = workers.map(w => {
        const stateColor = w.state === "busy" ? "var(--and-yellow)" : "var(--and-green)";
        const queueList  = (w.queues || []).join(", ") || w.worker_type || "worker";
        return `
        <div class="and-worker-tile">
          <div class="and-worker-pid text-blue">PID ${w.pid || "—"}</div>
          <div style="color:${stateColor};font-size:10px;font-weight:600">${w.state || "idle"}</div>
          <div class="and-worker-mem text-muted">${w.memory_mb || 0} MB</div>
          <div style="color:var(--and-green);font-size:10px" title="${w.name || ""}">${queueList}</div>
        </div>`;
      });

      const queueTiles = queues.map(q => `
        <div class="and-worker-tile" style="border-color:var(--and-yellow)">
          <div style="color:var(--and-yellow);font-size:10px;font-weight:600">QUEUE</div>
          <div class="and-worker-pid text-blue">${q.name}</div>
          <div class="and-worker-mem text-muted">${q.count} job${q.count !== 1 ? "s" : ""}</div>
        </div>`);

      $("#and-workers-grid").html([...workerTiles, ...queueTiles].join(""));
    } catch (_) {
      $("#and-workers-grid").html(`<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">Unavailable</span>`);
    }
  }

  // ── 6.5 RQ Queue Monitor ──────────────────────────────────
  async _loadQueues() {
    try {
      // Try Go /metrics endpoint first, fallback to Frappe API
      const resp = await fetch(`http://${location.hostname}:${this._METRICS_PORT}/metrics`).catch(() => null);
      let queues = { default: 0, short: 0, long: 0 };

      if (resp && resp.ok) {
        const text = await resp.text();
        ["default", "short", "long"].forEach(q => {
          const m = text.match(new RegExp(`frappe_rq_queue_depth{queue="${q}"} (\\d+)`));
          if (m) queues[q] = parseInt(m[1]);
        });
      }

      const maxDepth = Math.max(...Object.values(queues), 1);
      const rows = Object.entries(queues).map(([name, depth]) => `
        <div class="and-queue-row">
          <span class="and-queue-name">${name}</span>
          <div class="and-queue-track">
            <div class="and-queue-fill" style="width:${Math.max(depth / maxDepth * 100, depth > 0 ? 4 : 0)}%"></div>
          </div>
          <span class="and-queue-count ${depth > 100 ? "text-red" : depth > 20 ? "text-yellow" : "text-green"}">${depth}</span>
        </div>`).join("");

      $("#and-queue-card").html(`
        <div class="and-card-header">
          <span class="and-card-title">RQ Queue Depths</span>
          <span class="and-card-icon">⚡</span>
        </div>
        ${rows}
      `);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ── 6.6 Security Audit Panel ──────────────────────────────
  async _loadSecurityAudit() {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch(
        `http://${location.hostname}:7070/audit/query?limit=8&severity=high`
      ).catch(() => null);

      if (!resp || !resp.ok) {
        $("#and-sec-list").html(`<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">Go sidecar offline — start with: <code>./go/frappe_rpg_scout</code></span>`);
        return;
      }

      const events = await resp.json();
      if (!events || !events.length) {
        $("#and-sec-list").html(`<span class="text-green" style="font-size:12px">✅ No security events recorded</span>`);
        return;
      }

      const rows = events.map(e => `
        <div class="and-sec-event">
          <span class="and-sec-badge">${e.severity || "ALERT"}</span>
          <div>
            <div>${frappe.utils.escape_html(e.event_type || e.type || "security_event")}</div>
            <div class="and-sec-detail">${frappe.utils.escape_html(e.detail || JSON.stringify(e.payload || {}).slice(0, 80))}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;color:var(--and-muted);flex-shrink:0">
            ${e.timestamp ? new Date(e.timestamp * 1000).toLocaleTimeString() : ""}
          </div>
        </div>`).join("");

      $("#and-sec-list").html(rows);
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ── RPG Performance Panels ────────────────────────────────
  async _loadRpgStatus() {

  }

  async _loadRpgCacheHitRate() {
    try {
      const r = await frappe.call({ method: "frappe_rpg.api_perf.get_cache_hit_rate" });
      const d = r.message || {};
      const hits   = d.hits   || 0;
      const misses = d.misses || 0;
      const total  = hits + misses;
      const pct    = total > 0 ? ((hits / total) * 100).toFixed(1) : "—";
      const color  = total > 0 && (hits/total) > 0.8 ? "var(--and-green)" : "var(--and-accent)";
      $("#and-rpg-cache-body").html(`
        <div style="font-size:32px;font-weight:700;color:${color};text-align:center;padding:8px 0">${pct}%</div>

          ${hits.toLocaleString()} hits · ${misses.toLocaleString()} misses · backend: ${d.backend || "unknown"}
        </div>`);
    } catch(e) {
      $("#and-rpg-cache-body").html(`<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">⚠️ ${e}</span>`);
    }
  }

  async _loadRpgSlowApis() {
    try {
      const r = await frappe.call({ method: "frappe_rpg.api_perf.get_slow_apis", args: { limit: 8 } });
      const rows = (r.message || []);
      if (!rows.length) {
        $("#and-rpg-slow-body").html(`<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">No data yet — requires frappe_rs compiled.</span>`);
        return;
      }
      const html = rows.map(row => {
        const ms = row.avg_us ? (row.avg_us / 1000).toFixed(1) + " ms" : (row.ts || "");
        const color = (row.avg_us || 0) > 1000000 ? "var(--and-red)" :
                      (row.avg_us || 0) > 200000  ? "var(--and-accent)" : "var(--and-muted)";
        return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--and-border);font-size:12px">
          <span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${frappe.utils.escape_html(row.op || row.title || "—")}</span>
          <span style="color:${color};font-weight:600;margin-left:12px;flex-shrink:0">${ms}</span>
        </div>`;
      }).join("");
      $("#and-rpg-slow-body").html(html);
    } catch(e) {
      $("#and-rpg-slow-body").html(`<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">⚠️ ${e}</span>`);
    }
  }

  _renderError(id, msg) {
    $("#" + id).find(".and-shimmer").replaceWith(
      `<span class="text-muted" style="font-size:12px">⚠️ ${msg}</span>`
    );
  }

})();

  if (document.getElementById("and-fonts")) return;
  const l = document.createElement("link");
  l.id = "and-fonts";
  l.rel = "stylesheet";
  l.href = "https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap";
  document.head.appendChild(l);
})();

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/herd_guard.py
"""
frappe_rpg.herd_guard
======================
Thundering-herd protection for @frappe.whitelist() API functions.

Problem
-------
When the Redis cache is cold (first request of the day, or after a cache
flush) and 10+ users simultaneously hit the same expensive API endpoint,
all 10 workers execute the full computation in parallel — multiplying DB
load and external-API calls by 10×.

DmIqService already has this protection for its own HTTP calls.  This
module brings the same guarantee to any Frappe whitelist API.

How it works
------------
Uses Redis SET NX (atomic "set if not exists") as a distributed lock.
The first worker to acquire the lock does the real work and writes the
result to the app-level cache.  All other concurrent workers spin-wait
(with exponential back-off) on the Redis result key.  As soon as the
first worker populates the cache, the waiting workers read it and return
immediately without re-executing the computation.

  worker 1 → acquires lock → computes → sets cache → releases lock
  workers 2-9 → miss lock → wait → read cache set by worker 1

If the lock holder crashes or times out, the lock expires (TTL = 90s)
and the next worker retakes it.

Usage — @herd_protected decorator
-----------------------------------
    from frappe_rpg.herd_guard import herd_protected

    @herd_protected(cache_ttl=86400, lock_ttl=90)
    @frappe.whitelist()
    def get_sales_persons_details(fiscal_year, from_date, to_date, ...):
        ...  # expensive computation

    # Works transparently — if frappe_rpg is not installed the decorator
    # is a no-op pass-through.

Usage — manual (wrap any callable)
------------------------------------
    from frappe_rpg.herd_guard import guarded_call

    result = guarded_call(
        cache_key="my_api:employee_123:2026",
        compute_fn=lambda: expensive_fn(employee_id, fiscal_year),
        cache_ttl=86400,
    )
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time
import uuid
from functools import wraps
from typing import Any, Callable

ENABLED = True

_DEFAULT_LOCK_TTL  = 90     # seconds before a stalled lock auto-expires
_DEFAULT_WAIT_SECS = 85     # how long a waiter polls before giving up
_DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL = 86400  # 24 h — matches DmIqService default


# ── Core guarded call ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def guarded_call(
	cache_key: str,
	compute_fn: Callable[[], Any],
	cache_ttl: int = _DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL,
	lock_ttl: int  = _DEFAULT_LOCK_TTL,
	wait_secs: int = _DEFAULT_WAIT_SECS,
) -> Any:
	"""
	Return the cached result for *cache_key*, computing it exactly once even
	when many concurrent callers arrive simultaneously on a cold cache.

	Args:
	    cache_key:   A unique string identifying this computation.
	    compute_fn:  Zero-argument callable that produces the result.
	    cache_ttl:   How long (seconds) to cache the computed result.
	    lock_ttl:    How long (seconds) before a stalled lock auto-expires.
	    wait_secs:   How long (seconds) a non-lock-holder waits for the result.

	Returns:
	    The cached or freshly computed result.  Returns ``compute_fn()``
	    directly (bypassing the guard) if Redis is unavailable.
	"""
	if not ENABLED:
		return compute_fn()

	try:
		import frappe
		cache = frappe.cache
	except Exception:
		return compute_fn()

	# ── 1. Fast path: result already cached ───────────────────────────────
	cached = _cache_get(cache, cache_key)
	if cached is not None:
		return cached

	# ── 2. Try to acquire the distributed lock ────────────────────────────
	lock_key   = f"{cache_key}:__herd_lock__"
	lock_token = str(uuid.uuid4())

	try:
		acquired = cache.set(
			name=cache.make_key(lock_key),
			value=lock_token,
			ex=lock_ttl,
			nx=True,   # only set if key does not exist
		)
	except Exception:
		# Redis unavailable — fall through to direct compute
		return compute_fn()

	if acquired:
		# ── 3a. We hold the lock — compute and populate cache ─────────────
		try:
			result = compute_fn()
			_cache_set(cache, cache_key, result, cache_ttl)
			return result
		finally:
			_release_lock(cache, lock_key, lock_token)
	else:
		# ── 3b. Another worker holds the lock — wait for their result ─────
		result = _wait_for_result(cache, cache_key, lock_key, wait_secs)
		if result is not None:
			return result
		# Lock holder timed out or crashed — compute ourselves as fallback
		return compute_fn()


# ── Decorator ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def herd_protected(
	cache_ttl: int = _DEFAULT_CACHE_TTL,
	lock_ttl: int  = _DEFAULT_LOCK_TTL,
	wait_secs: int = _DEFAULT_WAIT_SECS,
	key_fn: Callable | None = None,
):
	"""
	Decorator that wraps a @frappe.whitelist() function with herd protection
	and response caching.

	The cache key is built from the function's module path + all arguments
	unless *key_fn* is supplied.

	    @herd_protected(cache_ttl=86400)
	    @frappe.whitelist()
	    def get_sales_persons_details(fiscal_year, from_date, ...):
	        ...

	When the underlying cache already has a result (e.g. from the
	app-level _set_cache() call inside the function) the herd guard reads
	it and returns immediately without calling the function at all.

	No-op when frappe_rpg is not installed or ENABLED=False.
	"""
	def decorator(fn):
		@wraps(fn)
		def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
			if not ENABLED:
				return fn(*args, **kwargs)

			if key_fn is not None:
				ck = key_fn(*args, **kwargs)
			else:
				parts = [fn.__module__, fn.__qualname__, *args,
				         *[f"{k}={v}" for k, v in sorted(kwargs.items())]]
				ck = "herd:" + ":".join(str(p) for p in parts)

			return guarded_call(
				cache_key=ck,
				compute_fn=lambda: fn(*args, **kwargs),
				cache_ttl=cache_ttl,
				lock_ttl=lock_ttl,
				wait_secs=wait_secs,
			)

		wrapper.__herd_wrapped__ = True
		return wrapper
	return decorator


# ── Private helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _cache_get(cache, key: str) -> Any:
	try:
		return cache.get_value(key)
	except Exception:
		return None


def _cache_set(cache, key: str, value: Any, ttl: int) -> None:
	try:
		cache.set_value(key, value, expires_in_sec=ttl)
	except Exception:
		pass


def _release_lock(cache, lock_key: str, token: str) -> None:
	try:
		rkey = cache.make_key(lock_key)
		stored = cache.get(rkey)
		if stored and stored.decode() == token:
			cache.delete(rkey)
	except Exception:
		pass


def _wait_for_result(cache, result_key: str, lock_key: str, wait_secs: int) -> Any:
	"""Poll *result_key* until the lock holder populates it or timeout."""
	deadline = time.perf_counter() + wait_secs
	sleep = 0.1
	while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
		time.sleep(sleep)
		cached = _cache_get(cache, result_key)
		if cached is not None:
			return cached
		# Stop waiting if the lock has already been released
		try:
			if not cache.get(cache.make_key(lock_key)):
				cached = _cache_get(cache, result_key)
				return cached  # may still be None if lock holder failed
		except Exception:
			pass
		sleep = min(sleep * 1.5, 2.0)
	return None

# Request Events
# Order matters:
#   1. auto_wire   — installs Rust-LRU patches on all frappe data functions
#   2. request_memo — installs per-request memoization patches + resets memo dict
#   3. doctype_hooks — records request start time for latency tracking
before_request = [
	"frappe_rpg.auto_wire.before_request",
	"frappe_rpg.request_memo.before_request",
