# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/install.py
"""
frappe_rpg/install.py
=====================
Runs automatically when `bench install-app frappe_rpg` is executed.

Full zero-to-hero setup:
  1. Install Rust toolchain (rustup) if missing
  2. Install Go if missing (via brew on macOS or pkg on Linux)
  3. Install maturin into the bench venv
  4. Compile frappe_rs Rust extension (maturin develop)
  5. Verify the extension is importable
  6. Build the Go scout sidecar binary

No manual steps required by the developer.
"""

import os
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import urllib.request

# NOTE: do NOT import frappe at module level — install.py runs at
# `bench install-app` time, before Frappe is fully initialised.
# Any frappe calls must be deferred to function bodies.


# ─── Logging helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _title(msg: str):
	print(f"\n{'─' * 64}")
	print(f"  ⚔️  [frappe_rpg] {msg}")
	print(f"{'─' * 64}")


def _step(msg: str):
	print(f"\n  🔧 {msg}")


def _ok(msg: str):
	print(f"  ✅ {msg}")


def _warn(msg: str):
	print(f"  ⚠️  {msg}")


def _err(msg: str):
	print(f"  ❌ {msg}")


def _info(msg: str):
	print(f"     {msg}")


# ─── Subprocess helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _run(
	cmd: list[str],
	cwd: str | None = None,
	env: dict | None = None,
	stream: bool = False,
) -> tuple[int, str]:
	"""
	Run a command. If stream=True prints each line live (for long builds).
	Returns (returncode, combined_output).
	"""
	if stream:
		proc = subprocess.Popen(
			cmd, cwd=cwd, env=env,
			stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True,
		)
		# Assign to a local so Pyre2 narrows the type — `assert` is not
		# recognised as a narrowing construct by Pyre2.
		stdout = proc.stdout
		if stdout is None:
			proc.wait()
			return proc.returncode, ""
		lines = []
		for line in stdout:
			line = line.rstrip()
			print(f"     {line}")
			lines.append(line)
		proc.wait()
		return proc.returncode, "\n".join(lines)
	else:
		result = subprocess.run(
			cmd, cwd=cwd, env=env,
			stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True,
		)
		return result.returncode, result.stdout.strip()


# ─── Path helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _app_root() -> str:
	"""Root of the frappe_rpg app (contains rust/, go/, frappe_rpg/)."""
	return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def _rust_dir() -> str:
	return os.path.join(_app_root(), "rust")


def _go_dir() -> str:
	return os.path.join(_app_root(), "go")


def _bench_python() -> str:
	return sys.executable


def _bench_venv() -> str:
	"""VIRTUAL_ENV — parent of bin/python."""
	return os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(sys.executable))


def _bench_bin(name: str) -> str:
	"""Path to an executable inside the bench venv bin/."""
	return os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sys.executable), name)


def _system() -> str:
	return platform.system()  # "Darwin" | "Linux" | "Windows"


# ─── Step 1: Install Rust ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _source_cargo_env() -> dict:
	"""
	Return an environ that includes $HOME/.cargo/bin on PATH.
	This is needed immediately after rustup installs without shell reload.
	"""
	env = os.environ.copy()
	cargo_bin = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cargo", "bin")
	if cargo_bin not in env.get("PATH", ""):
		env["PATH"] = cargo_bin + os.pathsep + env.get("PATH", "")
	return env


def _ensure_rust() -> bool:
	"""Install Rust via rustup if cargo is not found."""
	_step("Checking Rust toolchain (cargo)...")

	env = _source_cargo_env()

	# Already installed?
	cargo = shutil.which("cargo") or shutil.which(
		"cargo", path=os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cargo", "bin")
	)
	if cargo:
		code, ver = _run([cargo, "--version"], env=env)
		_ok(f"Rust found: {ver}")
		return True

	_warn("Rust not found — installing via rustup...")

	if _system() == "Windows":
		_err("Auto-install not supported on Windows. Install Rust from https://rustup.rs")
		return False

	# Download and run rustup-init
	rustup_url = "https://sh.rustup.rs"
	_info(f"Downloading rustup from {rustup_url}...")

	try:
		with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".sh", delete=False, mode="wb") as f:
			tmp_path = f.name
			with urllib.request.urlopen(rustup_url) as resp:
				f.write(resp.read())

		os.chmod(tmp_path, 0o755)

		_info("Running rustup-init (non-interactive, default profile)...")
		code, out = _run(
			["sh", tmp_path, "-y", "--default-toolchain", "stable", "--profile", "default"],
			stream=True,
		)
		os.unlink(tmp_path)

		if code != 0:
			_err(f"rustup-init failed (exit {code})")
			return False

		# Verify it's now available
		env = _source_cargo_env()
		cargo = shutil.which("cargo", path=env["PATH"])
		if not cargo:
			_err("cargo still not found after rustup install. Source $HOME/.cargo/env and retry.")
			return False

		code, ver = _run([cargo, "--version"], env=env)
		_ok(f"Rust installed: {ver}")
		return True

	except Exception as exc:
		_err(f"Rust installation failed: {exc}")
		return False


# ─── Step 2: Install Go ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Official Go release URL — update version here as needed
GO_VERSION = "1.22.3"
GO_DOWNLOAD_URLS = {
	"Darwin_arm64":  f"https://go.dev/dl/go{GO_VERSION}.darwin-arm64.tar.gz",
	"Darwin_x86_64": f"https://go.dev/dl/go{GO_VERSION}.darwin-amd64.tar.gz",
	"Linux_x86_64":  f"https://go.dev/dl/go{GO_VERSION}.linux-amd64.tar.gz",
	"Linux_aarch64": f"https://go.dev/dl/go{GO_VERSION}.linux-arm64.tar.gz",
}


def _ensure_go() -> bool:
	"""Install Go if not present. Uses brew on macOS, tar.gz on Linux."""
	_step("Checking Go toolchain...")

	go_bin = shutil.which("go")
	if go_bin:
		code, ver = _run([go_bin, "version"])
		_ok(f"Go found: {ver}")
		return True

	_warn(f"Go not found — installing Go {GO_VERSION}...")

	sys_name = _system()

	# macOS — try brew first (faster, managed updates)
	if sys_name == "Darwin":
		brew = shutil.which("brew")
		if brew:
			_info("Installing via Homebrew...")
			code, out = _run([brew, "install", "go"], stream=True)
			if code == 0:
				go_bin = shutil.which("go")
				if go_bin:
					code, ver = _run([go_bin, "version"])
					_ok(f"Go installed via brew: {ver}")
					return True

		_info("brew not found or failed — falling back to tar.gz install")

	# Linux + macOS fallback — download tar.gz from go.dev
	arch = platform.machine()  # arm64, x86_64, aarch64
	key = f"{sys_name}_{arch}"
	url = GO_DOWNLOAD_URLS.get(key)

	if not url:
		_warn(
			f"No pre-built Go binary URL for {key}.\n"
			"     Install Go manually from https://go.dev/doc/install\n"
			"     The Rust extension still works — Go sidecar is optional for Phase 1."
		)
		return False  # Non-fatal

	install_dir = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "go_install")
	os.makedirs(install_dir, exist_ok=True)
	tar_path = os.path.join(install_dir, f"go{GO_VERSION}.tar.gz")

	_info(f"Downloading {url}...")
	try:
		urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, tar_path)
	except Exception as exc:
		_warn(f"Go download failed: {exc}. Skipping — Go is optional for Phase 1.")
		return False

	_info(f"Extracting to {install_dir}...")
	code, out = _run(["tar", "-C", install_dir, "-xzf", tar_path])
	if code != 0:
		_warn(f"Go extraction failed: {out}. Skipping.")
		return False

	go_bin = os.path.join(install_dir, "go", "bin", "go")
	if not os.path.isfile(go_bin):
		_warn("Go binary not found after extraction. Skipping.")
		return False

	# Add to current process PATH
	go_bin_dir = os.path.dirname(go_bin)
	if go_bin_dir not in os.environ.get("PATH", ""):
		os.environ["PATH"] = go_bin_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ.get("PATH", "")

	code, ver = _run([go_bin, "version"])
	_ok(f"Go installed: {ver}")
	_info(f"Add to your shell profile: export PATH=\"{go_bin_dir}:$PATH\"")
	return True


# ─── Step 3: Install maturin ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _ensure_maturin() -> bool:
	"""Install maturin into the bench venv."""
	_step("Checking maturin (PyO3 build tool)...")

	# Check if already installed
	code, _ = _run([_bench_python(), "-m", "maturin", "--version"])
	if code == 0:
		code, ver = _run([_bench_python(), "-m", "maturin", "--version"])
		_ok(f"maturin already installed: {ver}")
		return True

	_info("Installing maturin into bench venv...")
	pip = _bench_bin("pip")
	code, out = _run([pip, "install", "maturin>=1.5,<2.0"])
	if code != 0:
		_err(f"maturin install failed:\n{out}")
		return False

	code, ver = _run([_bench_python(), "-m", "maturin", "--version"])
	_ok(f"maturin installed: {ver}")
	return True


# ─── Step 4: Compile Rust extension ───────────────────────────────────────────

def _build_rust_extension() -> bool:
	"""Run `maturin develop` to compile frappe_rs.abi3.so into the bench venv."""
	_step("Compiling Rust extension — frappe_rs (PyO3/maturin)...")
	_info("First build downloads Cargo deps — may take 60–120s...")

	rust_path = _rust_dir()
	if not os.path.isdir(rust_path):
		_err(f"rust/ directory not found at {rust_path}")
		return False

	# Prefer the venv-local maturin binary
	maturin_bin = _bench_bin("maturin") or shutil.which("maturin")
	if not maturin_bin:
		_err("maturin binary not found after install")
		return False

	env = _source_cargo_env()
	env["VIRTUAL_ENV"] = _bench_venv()

	code, _ = _run([maturin_bin, "develop"], cwd=rust_path, env=env, stream=True)

	if code != 0:
		_err("Rust extension build FAILED — see output above")
		return False

	_ok("frappe_rs compiled and installed into bench venv")
	return True


def _verify_rust_extension() -> bool:
	"""Quick smoke-test: import frappe_rs and call fast_sum."""
	_step("Verifying frappe_rs is importable...")
	code, out = _run([
		_bench_python(), "-c",
		"from frappe_rs import fast_sum, version_info; "
		"assert fast_sum(3, 4) == 7, 'fast_sum broken'; "
		"info = version_info(); "
		"print(info['version'], '|', info['cpu_threads'], 'threads')"
	])
	if code == 0:
		_ok(f"frappe_rs verified — {out.strip()}")
		return True
	_err(f"frappe_rs verification failed:\n{out}")
	return False


# ─── Step 5: Build Go sidecar ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _build_go_sidecar() -> bool:
	"""Compile the Go scout sidecar binary."""
	_step("Building Go scout sidecar...")

	go_bin = shutil.which("go")
	if not go_bin:
		_warn("Go not found on PATH — skipping sidecar build (non-fatal for Phase 1)")
		return True

	go_path = _go_dir()
	if not os.path.isdir(go_path):
		_warn(f"go/ directory not found at {go_path} — skipping")
		return True

	out_bin = os.path.join(go_path, "frappe_rpg_scout")
	code, out = _run([go_bin, "build", "-o", out_bin, "./sidecar/"], cwd=go_path)

	if code != 0:
		_warn(f"Go sidecar build failed (non-fatal):\n{out}")
		return True

	_ok(f"Go sidecar compiled → {out_bin}")
	return True


# ─── Main entry points ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _ensure_wizard_deps() -> bool:
	"""Install questionary + rich for the TUI wizard."""
	_step("Installing TUI wizard dependencies (questionary, rich)...")
	pip = _bench_bin("pip")
	code, out = _run([pip, "install", "questionary>=2.0", "rich>=13.0", "-q"])
	if code == 0:
		_ok("questionary + rich installed")
		return True
	_warn(f"Could not install TUI deps (non-fatal): {out}")
	return False


def _ensure_fastapi_lane_deps() -> bool:
	"""Install FastAPI lane runtime deps (Phase 44).

	These are also declared in pyproject.toml `dependencies` so a clean
	`bench install-app frappe_rpg` should pull them automatically. This step
	is a safety net for bench versions that skip pyproject deps or for
	manual re-runs.
	"""
	_step("Installing FastAPI lane dependencies (Phase 44)...")
	pip = _bench_bin("pip")
	deps = [
		"fastapi>=0.115",
		"uvicorn[standard]>=0.30",
		"pydantic>=2.5",
		"httpx>=0.27",
		"sse-starlette>=2.1",
	]
	code, out = _run([pip, "install", *deps, "-q"])
	if code == 0:
		_ok("FastAPI lane deps installed (fastapi, uvicorn, pydantic, httpx, sse-starlette)")
		_info("Start the lane: env/bin/uvicorn frappe_rpg.fastapi_lane.app:app --port 7094")
		return True
	_warn(f"Could not install FastAPI lane deps (non-fatal — endpoints at :7094 will be unavailable):\n{out}")
	return False


def _run_wizard():
	"""Launch the interactive configuration TUI."""
	# Skip in non-interactive / CI environments
	if not sys.stdin.isatty():
		_info("Non-interactive terminal detected — skipping TUI wizard.")
		_info("Re-run anytime: bench execute frappe_rpg.setup_wizard.run_wizard")
		return

	_step("Launching interactive feature configuration wizard...")
	_info("Configuring optional features (DuckDB, DynamoDB, S3, Webhooks, Prometheus...)")

	try:
		# Relative import works when called as a Frappe hook (package context).
		# Absolute fallback handles `python install.py` direct invocation.
		try:
			from .setup_wizard import run_wizard  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		except ImportError:
			from frappe_rpg.setup_wizard import run_wizard  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		run_wizard()
	except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
		_warn("Wizard cancelled. Re-run: bench execute frappe_rpg.setup_wizard.run_wizard")
	except Exception as exc:
		_warn(f"Wizard failed (non-fatal): {exc}")
		_info("Re-run manually: bench execute frappe_rpg.setup_wizard.run_wizard")


# ─── Main entry points ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def after_install():
	"""
	Called by `bench install-app frappe_rpg`.

	Performs full zero-to-hero setup:
	  Step 1 — Install Rust toolchain (if missing)
	  Step 2 — Install Go toolchain (if missing)
	  Step 3 — Install maturin into bench venv
	  Step 4 — Compile frappe_rs Rust extension
	  Step 5 — Build Go scout sidecar binary
	  Step 6 — Install FastAPI lane deps (Phase 44)
	  Step 7 — Run interactive TUI configuration wizard
	"""
	print("\n")
	print("=" * 64)
	print("  ⚔️  Frappe-RPG — Auto Setup")
	print("  Rust · Python/PyO3 · Go · TUI Config")
	print("=" * 64)

	errors = []

	# 1. Rust
	_title("Step 1/7 — Rust Toolchain")
	if not _ensure_rust():
		errors.append("Rust toolchain installation failed")

	# 2. Go (non-fatal)
	_title("Step 2/7 — Go Toolchain")
	_ensure_go()

	# 3. maturin
	_title("Step 3/7 — maturin (PyO3 build tool)")
	if not errors:
		if not _ensure_maturin():
			errors.append("maturin installation failed")

	# 4. Build Rust extension
	_title("Step 4/7 — Compiling Rust Extension (frappe_rs)")
	if not errors:
		if not _build_rust_extension():
			errors.append("frappe_rs Rust extension failed to compile")
		elif not _verify_rust_extension():
			errors.append("frappe_rs import verification failed")

	# 5. Go sidecar
	_title("Step 5/7 — Go Scout Sidecar")
	_build_go_sidecar()

	# 6. FastAPI lane (Phase 44) — non-fatal; lane is optional at :7094
	_title("Step 6/7 — FastAPI Lane Dependencies (Phase 44)")
	_ensure_fastapi_lane_deps()

	# Summary so far
	print("\n" + "=" * 64)
	if errors:
		print("  ⚠️  Build completed WITH ERRORS — skipping wizard:")
		for err in errors:
			print(f"  ❌  {err}")
		print()
		print("  Manual fix:")
		print("      cd apps/frappe_rpg/rust")
		print("      VIRTUAL_ENV=../../../env ../../../env/bin/maturin develop")
		print("=" * 64 + "\n")
		return  # Don't run wizard if build failed

	print("  ✅  All binaries compiled successfully!")
	print("=" * 64)

	# 7. TUI wizard for optional feature config
	_title("Step 7/7 — Interactive Configuration Wizard")
	_ensure_wizard_deps()
	_run_wizard()

	# Final banner
	print("\n" + "=" * 64)
	print("  🏆  Frappe-RPG setup COMPLETE — The Warrior is live!")
	print()
	print("  Quick verification:")
	print("      bench console")
	print("      >>> from frappe_rs import fast_sum")
	print("      >>> fast_sum(5, 7)  # → 12")
	print()
	print("  Docs:  apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/docs/")
	print("  Guide: apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/docs/user_guide.md")
	print("=" * 64 + "\n")



def after_uninstall():
	"""Called by `bench uninstall-app frappe_rpg`. Removes frappe_rs from the venv."""
	_step("Removing frappe_rs from bench venv...")
	pip = _bench_bin("pip")
	code, out = _run([pip, "uninstall", "-y", "frappe_rs"])
	if code == 0:
		_ok("frappe_rs uninstalled from venv")
	else:
		_warn(f"Could not uninstall frappe_rs: {out}")

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/integrations/cache_bridge.py
"""
frappe_rpg.integrations.cache_bridge
======================================
Drop-in replacement for ``frappe.cache()`` that routes through the
Rust moka LRU cache (Phase 9) for hot documents.

Usage in hooks.py (doc_events) or anywhere in Frappe code:

    from frappe_rpg.integrations.cache_bridge import RpgCache

    cache = RpgCache()
    cache.set_value("my_key", {"data": 123}, expires_in_sec=300)
    value = cache.get_value("my_key")
    cache.delete_key("my_key")

The bridge transparently falls back to ``frappe.cache()`` when
frappe_rs is unavailable.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time
from datetime import date, datetime

try:
	import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	_RUST_OK = True
except ImportError:
	_rs = None
	_RUST_OK = False

try:
	import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	_FRAPPE_OK = True
except ImportError:
	frappe = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
	_FRAPPE_OK = False


class RpgCache:
	"""
	Unified cache that prefers the Rust LRU (sub-microsecond warm reads)
	and falls back to frappe.cache() (Redis).

	TTL is enforced in the Rust layer via moka's time-to-live support;
	the Python layer stores an ``_expires`` sentinel for the Redis fallback.

	Rust API (current):
	    cache_doc(doctype, name, json, ttl_secs)
	    get_cached_doc(doctype, name) -> str
	    invalidate_doc(doctype, name)
	"""

	# ── Write ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	def set_value(
		self,
		key: str,
		value: object,
		expires_in_sec: int = 3600,
		*,
		doctype: str | None = None,
		name: str | None = None,
	) -> None:
		"""Store *value* (JSON-serialisable) under *key*.

		If *doctype* and *name* are given the Rust cache is keyed by
		(doctype, name) directly via ``cache_doc``.
		"""
		# Use frappe's JSON encoder if available (handles datetime, Decimal, etc.)
		if _FRAPPE_OK:
			try:
				serialised = frappe.as_json(value)
			except Exception:
				serialised = json.dumps(value, default=str)
		else:
			serialised = json.dumps(value, default=str)

		if _RUST_OK and doctype and name:
			try:
				_rs.cache_doc(doctype, name, serialised, expires_in_sec)
			except Exception:
				pass

		if _FRAPPE_OK and frappe.cache():
			frappe.cache().set_value(key, value, expires_in_sec=expires_in_sec)

	# ── Read ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	def get_value(
		self,
		key: str,
		default: object = None,
		*,
		doctype: str | None = None,
		name: str | None = None,
	) -> object:
		"""Return cached value or *default* on cache miss."""
		# 1. Try Rust LRU (fastest path) when doctype+name available
		if _RUST_OK and doctype and name:
			try:
				raw = _rs.get_cached_doc(doctype, name)
				if raw:
					try:
						return json.loads(raw)
					except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
						return raw
			except Exception:
				pass

		# 2. Fall back to frappe.cache() (Redis)
		if _FRAPPE_OK and frappe.cache():
			return frappe.cache().get_value(key, default)

		return default

	# ── Delete ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	def delete_key(
		self,
		key: str,
		*,
		doctype: str | None = None,
		name: str | None = None,
	) -> None:
		if _RUST_OK and doctype and name:
			try:
				_rs.invalidate_doc(doctype, name)
			except Exception:
				pass

		if _FRAPPE_OK and frappe.cache():
			frappe.cache().delete_key(key)

	# ── Stats ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

	def stats(self) -> dict:
		"""Return cache statistics (hit rate, size, backend)."""
		result: dict = {"backend": "none"}

		if _RUST_OK:
			try:
				raw = _rs.cache_stats()
				result = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
				result["backend"] = "rust_lru"
			except Exception:
				pass

		return result

	# ── Context manager compatibility (matches frappe.cache() pattern) ────

	def __enter__(self):
		return self

	def __exit__(self, *_):
		pass


# ── Module-level singleton ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_default_cache = RpgCache()


def get_cache() -> RpgCache:
	"""Return the module-level RpgCache singleton."""
	return _default_cache

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/integrations/doctype_hooks.py
"""
frappe_rpg.integrations.doctype_hooks
========================================
Frappe ``doc_events`` handlers wired in ``hooks.py``.

Each handler is a plain Python function that Frappe calls during the
document lifecycle.  All heavy work is delegated to frappe_rs Rust
functions or the RpgCache bridge — Frappe is only used to read the
doc object.

Registered in hooks.py:
    doc_events = {
        "*": {
            "after_insert":  "frappe_rpg.integrations.doctype_hooks.after_insert",
            "on_update":     "frappe_rpg.integrations.doctype_hooks.on_update",
            "after_load":    "frappe_rpg.integrations.doctype_hooks.after_load",
            "on_trash":      "frappe_rpg.integrations.doctype_hooks.on_trash",
        }
    }
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import time

from frappe_rpg.auto_wire import invalidate_list_cache
from frappe_rpg.integrations.cache_bridge import get_cache

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
    _RUST_OK = True
except ImportError:
    _rs = None
    _RUST_OK = False

try:
    import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
except ImportError:
    frappe = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

_cache = get_cache()

# ─── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _doc_to_json(doc) -> str:
    """Serialise a Frappe document to JSON string for Rust functions."""
    try:
        return json.dumps(doc.as_dict())
    except Exception:
        return "{}"


def _record_latency(op: str, start: float) -> None:
    if _RUST_OK:
        elapsed_us = int((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1_000_000)
        try:
            _rs.benchmark_frappe_op(op, elapsed_us)
        except Exception:
            pass


# ─── Document event handlers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

def after_insert(doc, method=None):
    """Cache the newly inserted document in the Rust LRU."""
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _cache.set_value(
        key="",
        value=doc.as_dict(),
        doctype=doc.doctype,
        name=doc.name,
    )
    _record_latency(f"after_insert:{doc.doctype}", t0)


def on_update(doc, method=None):
    """Refresh the Rust LRU cache entry after every save and flush related lists."""
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _cache.set_value(
        key="",
        value=doc.as_dict(),
        doctype=doc.doctype,
        name=doc.name,
    )
    # Any write to this doctype invalidates all cached get_list/get_all results
    # so subsequent reads never see stale data.
    invalidate_list_cache(doc.doctype)
    _record_latency(f"on_update:{doc.doctype}", t0)


def after_load(doc, method=None):
    """Warm the Rust LRU cache on every load (cache-on-read pattern)."""
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    # Only cache if the document isn't already warm (avoid redundant writes)
    existing = _cache.get_value("", doctype=doc.doctype, name=doc.name)
    if existing is None:
        _cache.set_value("", value=doc.as_dict(), doctype=doc.doctype, name=doc.name)
    _record_latency(f"after_load:{doc.doctype}", t0)


def on_trash(doc, method=None):
    """Evict the cache entry when a document is deleted and flush related lists."""
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    _cache.delete_key("", doctype=doc.doctype, name=doc.name)
    invalidate_list_cache(doc.doctype)
    _record_latency(f"on_trash:{doc.doctype}", t0)


# ─── Before-request hook (performance timing) ─────────────────────────────────

_request_start: float = 0.0


def before_request():
    """Record request start time for end-to-end latency tracking."""
    global _request_start
    _request_start = time.perf_counter()


def after_request():
    """Record end-to-end request latency in the Rust benchmark store."""
    if _RUST_OK and _request_start > 0:
        elapsed_us = int((time.perf_counter() - _request_start) * 1_000_000)
        try:
            _rs.benchmark_frappe_op("http_request", elapsed_us)
        except Exception:
            pass

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/integrations/permission_hook.py
"""Phase 29 — Permission Engine hook.

Wraps frappe.has_permission() with a Rust DashMap cache.  Cache miss falls
back to Frappe's own check, then populates the cache for subsequent calls.
"""

import json

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
    _PERM_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    _PERM_AVAILABLE = False


def has_permission(doc, ptype="read", user=None):
    """Drop-in replacement for frappe.has_permission().

    Returns True/False.  Falls back to Frappe on cache miss.
    """
    if not _PERM_AVAILABLE:
        import frappe
        return frappe.has_permission(doc.doctype, ptype, doc, user=user)

    import frappe

    user = user or frappe.session.user

    # Push current user's roles into Rust (cheap if already cached — Rust TTL guards)
    try:
        roles = frappe.get_roles(user)
        _rs.permission_set_user_roles(user, json.dumps(roles))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Try Rust cache
    try:
        result = json.loads(_rs.permission_check(user, doc.doctype, ptype))
        if result.get("cached"):
            return bool(result.get("allowed"))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Cache miss — use Frappe's own check
    allowed = frappe.has_permission(doc.doctype, ptype, doc, user=user)

    # Load doctype rules into Rust cache for next time
    try:
        _load_doctype_perms(doc.doctype)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return allowed


def _load_doctype_perms(doctype: str):
    """Fetch DocType permission rules from DB and push into Rust cache."""
    import frappe
    perms = frappe.db.get_all(
        "DocPerm",
        filters={"parent": doctype},
        fields=[
            "role", "read", "write", "create", "delete",
            "submit", "cancel", "amend", "report",
            "export", "import", "print", "email", "share",
        ],
    )
    if perms:
        _rs.permission_load_doctype(doctype, json.dumps(perms))


def on_doctype_update(doc, method=None):
    """Invalidate Rust permission cache when a DocType is saved."""
    if not _PERM_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        _rs.permission_invalidate(doc.name)
    except Exception:
        pass


def on_user_role_change(doc, method=None):
    """Evict user from Rust cache when their roles change."""
    if not _PERM_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        _rs.permission_clear_user(doc.name)
    except Exception:
        pass

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/integrations/response_hook.py
"""Phase 31 — Response hook.

For large JSON API responses, re-serialises through Rust to strip whitespace
and normalise encoding. Activated in hooks.py after_request.
"""

MIN_RESPONSE_BYTES = 4096  # only bother for responses larger than 4 KB

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
    _SIMD_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    _SIMD_AVAILABLE = False


def after_request(response=None):
    if not _SIMD_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        import frappe
        # Frappe passes the response via frappe.local.response for JSON endpoints
        resp = response or getattr(frappe.local, "response", None)
        if resp is None:
            return
        content_type = getattr(resp, "content_type", "") or ""
        if "application/json" not in content_type:
            return
        data = getattr(resp, "data", None)
        if data is None or len(data) < MIN_RESPONSE_BYTES:
            return
        if isinstance(data, bytes):
            data = data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        minified = _rs.json_minify_fast(data)
        resp.data = minified.encode("utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/integrations/session_hook.py
"""Phase 30 — Session Cache hook.

Stores session data and DocType metadata in Rust process memory to eliminate
Redis round-trips and repeated JSON parsing on every request.
"""

import json

try:
    import frappe_rs as _rs
    _SESSION_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    _SESSION_AVAILABLE = False


SESSION_TTL = 60       # seconds — mirrors Frappe's default session expiry behaviour
META_WARM_LIST = [
    "Sales Invoice", "Sales Order", "Purchase Order", "Purchase Invoice",
    "Customer", "Supplier", "Item", "Employee", "Journal Entry",
    "Stock Entry", "Delivery Note", "Payment Entry", "Quotation",
    "Lead", "Opportunity", "DocType", "User", "Role", "DocPerm",
]


def on_session_creation(login_manager):
    """Cache session data in Rust on login."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        import frappe
        sid = frappe.local.session_obj.sid
        data = frappe.as_json(frappe.local.session_data)
        _rs.session_set(sid, data, SESSION_TTL)
    except Exception:
        pass


def before_request():
    """Try to serve session from Rust cache; fall back to Redis on miss."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        import frappe
        cookies = getattr(frappe.local, "cookie_manager", None)
        if cookies is None:
            return
        sid = cookies.cookies.get("sid")
        if not sid or sid == "Guest":
            return
        result = json.loads(_rs.session_get(sid))
        if result.get("cached"):
            frappe.local._rpg_session_cache = result["data"]
            _rs.session_touch(sid)
    except Exception:
        pass


def on_session_destroy(login_manager):
    """Evict session from Rust cache on logout."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        import frappe
        sid = getattr(frappe.local, "session_obj", None)
        if sid:
            _rs.session_invalidate(sid.sid)
    except Exception:
        pass


# ── DocType meta helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def get_meta_cached(doctype: str):
    """Return cached meta dict or None on miss."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return None
    try:
        result = json.loads(_rs.meta_get(doctype))
        if result.get("cached"):
            return result["meta"]
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def set_meta_cached(doctype: str, meta_dict: dict):
    """Push a meta dict into the Rust cache."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        _rs.meta_set(doctype, json.dumps(meta_dict))
    except Exception:
        pass


def warm_meta_cache():
    """Pre-load common DocType metas at startup."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        import frappe
        metas = {}
        for dt in META_WARM_LIST:
            try:
                metas[dt] = frappe.get_meta(dt).as_dict()
            except Exception:
                pass
        if metas:
            _rs.meta_warm(json.dumps(metas))
    except Exception:
        pass


def on_doctype_update(doc, method=None):
    """Invalidate meta cache when a DocType is modified."""
    if not _SESSION_AVAILABLE:
        return
    try:
        _rs.meta_invalidate(doc.name)
    except Exception:
        pass

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/request_memo.py
"""
frappe_rpg.request_memo
========================
Per-request in-memory memoization for expensive Frappe calls.

Problem
-------
Within a single HTTP request many helpers call the same functions with the
same arguments repeatedly:

    _get_subordinate_ids(employee_id)   called 6+ times in get_data
    _get_employee_codes(subordinate_ids) called 3+ times
    _get_all_sb_budget_items(...)        called 4+ times
    frappe.get_cached_doc("DM Settings") called once per helper

Even with Rust LRU (sub-ms Redis), there is still Python-side overhead for
JSON deserialisation and dict lookups on every call.  A request-scoped dict
that lives only for the lifetime of one HTTP request collapses all duplicate
calls to zero-cost dictionary lookups.

How it works
------------
``frappe.local`` is a thread-local (or greenlet-local) object that Frappe
resets at the start of every request.  We attach a ``_rpg_memo`` dict to it.
The dict is created on first access and automatically discarded when Frappe
clears ``frappe.local`` at the end of the request — no manual cleanup needed.

Auto-wiring
-----------
``before_request`` (wired in hooks.py) installs patches on:
  - ``frappe.get_cached_doc``   → memo-wrapped
  - ``frappe.get_cached_value`` → memo-wrapped
  - ``frappe.get_single``       → memo-wrapped (settings singletons)

These sit *on top of* the Rust LRU patches from auto_wire.py so the call
order is: request memo → Rust LRU → Redis → DB.

Usage in custom code
--------------------
    from frappe_rpg.request_memo import memo_get, memo_set, memo_key

    key = memo_key("my_func", employee_id, fiscal_year)
    result = memo_get(key)
    if result is None:
        result = expensive_computation(...)
        memo_set(key, result)

    # Or use the decorator:
    from frappe_rpg.request_memo import request_cached

    @request_cached
    def _get_subordinate_ids(employee_id, is_individual=False):
        ...
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from functools import wraps
from typing import Any

_install_lock = threading.Lock()
_installed = False

ENABLED = True

_SENTINEL = object()  # distinguishes "key present, value=None" from "key absent"


# ── Low-level helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _memo_dict() -> dict:
	"""Return (or create) the per-request memo dict attached to frappe.local."""
	try:
		import frappe
		local = frappe.local
		if not hasattr(local, "_rpg_memo"):
			local._rpg_memo = {}
		return local._rpg_memo
	except Exception:
		return {}


def memo_key(*parts) -> str:
	"""Build a stable string key from arbitrary positional parts."""
	return "|".join(str(p) for p in parts)


def memo_get(key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
	"""Return the memoised value for *key*, or *default* on a miss."""
	d = _memo_dict()
	val = d.get(key, _SENTINEL)
	return default if val is _SENTINEL else val


def memo_set(key: str, value: Any) -> None:
	"""Store *value* under *key* for the duration of this request."""
	_memo_dict()[key] = value


def memo_clear() -> None:
	"""Evict all entries (called automatically at request teardown if needed)."""
	try:
		import frappe
		if hasattr(frappe.local, "_rpg_memo"):
			frappe.local._rpg_memo = {}
	except Exception:
		pass


# ── Decorator ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def request_cached(fn):
	"""
	Decorator that memoises a function's return value for the current request.

	The cache key is built from the function's qualified name and all positional
	and keyword arguments.  Works on module-level functions and instance methods.

	    @request_cached
	    def _get_subordinate_ids(employee_id, is_individual=False, **kw):
	        ...  # expensive hierarchy traversal

	Remove the decorator at any time — no functional change.
	"""
	@wraps(fn)
	def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
		k = memo_key(fn.__qualname__, *args, *sorted(kwargs.items()))
		val = memo_get(k, _SENTINEL)
		if val is not _SENTINEL:
			return val
		result = fn(*args, **kwargs)
		memo_set(k, result)
		return result

	wrapper.__memo_wrapped__ = True
	return wrapper


# ── Frappe built-in patches ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _install_patches() -> None:
	"""
	Wrap frappe.get_cached_doc, get_cached_value, get_single with request memo.
	Idempotent — safe to call on every request.
	"""
	global _installed

	if not ENABLED:
		return

	with _install_lock:
		if _installed:
			return

		import frappe as _f

		# ── get_cached_doc ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
		_orig_gcd = _f.get_cached_doc

		def _memo_get_cached_doc(doctype_or_meta, name=None, for_update=False):
			if isinstance(doctype_or_meta, str) and isinstance(name, str) and not for_update:
				k = memo_key("gcd", doctype_or_meta, name)
				val = memo_get(k, _SENTINEL)
				if val is not _SENTINEL:
					return val
				result = _orig_gcd(doctype_or_meta, name, for_update=for_update)
				memo_set(k, result)
				return result
			return _orig_gcd(doctype_or_meta, name, for_update=for_update)

		_memo_get_cached_doc.__memo_wrapped__ = True
		_memo_get_cached_doc.__wrapped__ = getattr(_orig_gcd, "__wrapped__", _orig_gcd)
		_f.get_cached_doc = _memo_get_cached_doc

		# ── get_cached_value ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
		_orig_gcv = _f.get_cached_value

		def _memo_get_cached_value(doctype, name, fieldname=None, as_dict=False):
			# Only memoise the (doctype, str-name, fieldname) form.
			# When name is a filter dict or fieldname is absent the call has a
			# different semantic — pass straight through to avoid breaking callers.
			if isinstance(doctype, str) and isinstance(name, str) and fieldname is not None:
				k = memo_key("gcv", doctype, name, str(fieldname), as_dict)
				val = memo_get(k, _SENTINEL)
				if val is not _SENTINEL:
					return val
				result = _orig_gcv(doctype, name, fieldname, as_dict=as_dict)
				memo_set(k, result)
				return result
			if fieldname is not None:
				return _orig_gcv(doctype, name, fieldname, as_dict=as_dict)
			return _orig_gcv(doctype, name)

		_memo_get_cached_value.__memo_wrapped__ = True
		_memo_get_cached_value.__wrapped__ = getattr(_orig_gcv, "__wrapped__", _orig_gcv)
		_f.get_cached_value = _memo_get_cached_value

		# ── get_single ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
		# Settings singletons (e.g. "DM Settings", "Budget Settings") are read
		# many times per request — memoising them saves repeated Redis hits.
		_orig_gs = _f.get_single

		def _memo_get_single(doctype):
			if isinstance(doctype, str):
				k = memo_key("gs", doctype)
				val = memo_get(k, _SENTINEL)
				if val is not _SENTINEL:
					return val
				result = _orig_gs(doctype)
				memo_set(k, result)
				return result
			return _orig_gs(doctype)

		_memo_get_single.__memo_wrapped__ = True
		_f.get_single = _memo_get_single

		_installed = True


# ── Frappe hook entry-points ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

def before_request() -> None:
	"""Install patches (once per process) and reset memo dict for this request."""
	if not _installed:
		_install_patches()
	# Reset the per-request dict so stale data from the previous request on
	# this worker never leaks into the current one.
	memo_clear()


def after_request() -> None:
	"""Clean up the memo dict after the request to release memory."""
	memo_clear()


# ── Status ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def status() -> dict:
	"""Diagnostic snapshot — use from bench console."""
	try:
		import frappe
		size = len(getattr(frappe.local, "_rpg_memo", {}))
	except Exception:
		size = -1

	return {
		"enabled": ENABLED,
		"installed": _installed,
		"current_request_entries": size,
	}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/setup_wizard.py
"""
frappe_rpg/setup_wizard.py
==========================
Interactive TUI configuration wizard — runs after the binary build phase
of `bench install-app frappe_rpg`.

Automatically detects what's already configured (Redis URL, existing
site_config entries) and only asks for things that are genuinely missing.

Requires: questionary, rich  (auto-installed if missing)
"""

import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys


# ─── Dependency bootstrap ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _ensure_wizard_deps():
	"""Silently pip-install questionary + rich if missing."""
	pip = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(sys.executable), "pip")
	for pkg, import_name in [("questionary", "questionary"), ("rich", "rich")]:
		try:
			import importlib
			importlib.import_module(import_name)
		except ImportError:
			subprocess.run(
				[pip, "install", pkg, "-q"],
				stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
				stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
			)


# ─── Site config helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _get_site_config(site: str) -> dict:
	"""Read site_config.json for the given site."""
	try:
		import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		bench_path = frappe.utils.get_bench_path()
		config_path = os.path.join(bench_path, "sites", site, "site_config.json")
		with open(config_path) as f:
			return json.load(f)
	except Exception:
		return {}


def _save_site_config(site: str, updates: dict):
	"""Merge updates into site_config.json."""
	try:
		import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		bench_path = frappe.utils.get_bench_path()
		config_path = os.path.join(bench_path, "sites", site, "site_config.json")
		with open(config_path) as f:
			config = json.load(f)
		config.update(updates)
		with open(config_path, "w") as f:
			json.dump(config, f, indent=2)
		return True
	except Exception as e:
		return False


def _get_current_sites() -> list[str]:
	"""List all bench sites."""
	try:
		import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		bench_path = frappe.utils.get_bench_path()
		sites_dir = os.path.join(bench_path, "sites")
		return [
			d for d in os.listdir(sites_dir)
			if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(sites_dir, d, "site_config.json"))
			and d not in ("assets", "apps.txt")
		]
	except Exception:
		return []

		return updates

		"Enable AWS DynamoDB (< 2ms hot-path KV reads, sessions, cache)?",
		default=False,
	).ask()

	if not enable:
		return updates

	console.print("\n  [bold cyan]AWS DynamoDB Setup[/bold cyan]")
	console.print("  [dim]Get these from AWS Console → IAM → Access Keys[/dim]\n")

	region = q.text(
		"  AWS Region:",
		default=site_config.get("aws_region", "ap-south-1"),
	).ask()

	access_key = q.password("  AWS Access Key ID:").ask()
	secret_key = q.password("  AWS Secret Access Key:").ask()

	table_prefix = q.text(
		"  DynamoDB table prefix (e.g. frappe-prod-):",
		default="frappe-rpg-",
	).ask()

	updates = {
		"aws_region": region,
		"aws_access_key_id": access_key,
		"aws_secret_access_key": secret_key,
		"dynamodb_table_prefix": table_prefix,
	}

	console.print("  [green]✅  DynamoDB configured[/green]")
	return updates

def _wizard_s3(q, console, site_config: dict) -> dict:
	"""Ask for S3-compatible object storage."""
	updates = {}

	if site_config.get("rpg_s3_bucket"):
		console.print("  [green]✅  S3 storage already configured[/green]")
		return updates

	enable = q.confirm(
		"Enable S3-compatible object storage (files, backups to S3/MinIO/R2)?",
		default=False,
	).ask()

	if not enable:
		return updates

	console.print("\n  [bold cyan]S3 Storage Setup[/bold cyan]")
	provider = q.select(
		"  Provider:",
		choices=["AWS S3", "Cloudflare R2", "MinIO (self-hosted)", "GCS (Google Cloud Storage)", "Other (custom endpoint)"],
	).ask()

	endpoint_url = ""
	if provider not in ("AWS S3", "GCS (Google Cloud Storage)"):
		endpoint_url = q.text("  Endpoint URL (e.g. https://minio.myserver.com):").ask()

	bucket = q.text("  Bucket name:").ask()
	region = q.text("  Region:", default="auto" if "R2" in provider else "us-east-1").ask()
	access_key = q.password("  Access Key ID:").ask()
	secret_key = q.password("  Secret Access Key:").ask()

	updates = {
		"rpg_s3_bucket": bucket,
		"rpg_s3_region": region,
		"rpg_s3_access_key": access_key,
		"rpg_s3_secret_key": secret_key,
	}
	if endpoint_url:
		updates["rpg_s3_endpoint"] = endpoint_url

	console.print(f"  [green]✅  S3 configured → s3://{bucket}[/green]")
	return updates

def _wizard_master_key(q, console, site_config: dict) -> dict:
	"""Generate or accept the AES-256 master encryption key."""
	updates = {}

	if site_config.get("rpg_master_key"):
		console.print("  [green]✅  Master encryption key already set[/green]")
		return updates

	import secrets
	auto_key = secrets.token_hex(32)

	console.print("\n  [bold cyan]Encryption Master Key[/bold cyan]")
	console.print("  [dim]Used to derive field-level AES-256-GCM encryption keys (Phase 4 Vault)[/dim]")

	choice = q.select(
		"  Master key:",
		choices=[
			f"Auto-generate (recommended): {auto_key:.16s}…",
			"Enter my own 64-hex-char key",
		],
	).ask()

	if "Auto-generate" in choice:
		key = auto_key
	else:
		key = q.password("  Enter 64-character hex key (32 bytes):").ask().strip()
		if len(key) != 64:
			console.print("  [red]Invalid key length — generating a random key instead[/red]")
			key = auto_key

	updates = {"rpg_master_key": key}
	console.print("  [green]✅  Master key saved to site_config.json[/green]")
	return updates


# ─── Site selector ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _pick_site(q, console) -> str | None:
	"""If multiple sites, ask which one to configure."""
	sites = _get_current_sites()

	if not sites:
		console.print("  [yellow]⚠️  No sites found. Run `bench new-site` first.[/yellow]")
		return None

	if len(sites) == 1:
		console.print(f"  [dim]Configuring site: {sites[0]}[/dim]")
		return sites[0]

	return q.select("  Which site to configure?", choices=sites).ask()


# ─── Main entry point ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def run_wizard():
	"""
	Main TUI wizard — called from install.py after the binary build phase.
	Skips sections that are already configured (idempotent).
	"""
	# Bootstrap deps silently
	_ensure_wizard_deps()

	import questionary as q  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	from rich.console import Console  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	from rich.rule import Rule  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
	from rich.text import Text  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

	console = Console()

	console.print()
	console.print(Rule(
		Text("⚔️  Frappe-RPG Configuration Wizard", style="bold yellow"),
		style="yellow",
	))
	console.print(
		"  Auto-detecting your setup. Only optional features need your input.\n"
		"  [dim]Press Enter to accept defaults. Ctrl+C to skip all optional config.[/dim]\n",
		style="dim"
	)

	# Pick site
	site = _pick_site(q, console)
	if not site:
		return

	site_config = _get_site_config(site)
	all_updates = {}

	sections = [
		("🔑  Master Encryption Key",     _wizard_master_key),
		("📊  DuckDB OLAP Engine",         _wizard_duckdb),
		("⚡  AWS DynamoDB (optional)",    _wizard_aws_dynamodb),
		("☁️   S3 Object Storage (opt.)",  _wizard_s3),
		("🔔  Security Webhooks (opt.)",   _wizard_webhook),
		("📈  Prometheus Metrics",         _wizard_prometheus),
	]

	try:
		for title, fn in sections:
			console.print(f"\n  [bold white]{title}[/bold white]")
			updates = fn(q, console, {**site_config, **all_updates})
			all_updates.update(updates)
	except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
		console.print("\n  [yellow]Wizard skipped — optional features not configured.[/yellow]")
		console.print("  [dim]Re-run anytime: bench execute frappe_rpg.setup_wizard.run_wizard[/dim]")

	# Save all collected settings
	if all_updates:
		if _save_site_config(site, all_updates):
			console.print()
			console.print(Rule(style="green"))
			console.print(f"  [bold green]✅  Configuration saved to site_config.json ({len(all_updates)} settings)[/bold green]")
		else:
			console.print("  [yellow]⚠️  Could not write to site_config.json — add settings manually[/yellow]")
			console.print(f"\n  Settings to add:\n  {json.dumps(all_updates, indent=4)}")
	else:
		console.print("\n  [dim]No changes needed — all settings already configured.[/dim]")

	console.print()
	console.print(Rule(
		Text("🏆  Frappe-RPG is Ready!", style="bold green"),
		style="green",
	))
	console.print(
		"  Re-run wizard anytime:\n"
		"  [bold]bench execute frappe_rpg.setup_wizard.run_wizard[/bold]\n",
	)

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/sql_router.py
"""
frappe_rpg.sql_router
======================
Transparently routes read-only ``frappe.db.sql`` calls through DuckDB
(in-process, Rust-backed) instead of MySQL for analytical queries.

Why DuckDB is faster for analytics
-----------------------------------
MySQL is a row-store optimised for OLTP (point lookups, small writes).
DuckDB is a columnar, vectorised OLAP engine.  For aggregation queries
(GROUP BY, SUM, COUNT DISTINCT, window functions) over large result sets,
DuckDB is typically 10-100× faster than MySQL because:

  - Column-level compression — only reads the columns referenced in SELECT
  - Vectorised execution — processes 1024 values per CPU instruction
  - Rayon parallelism — uses all CPU cores automatically
  - No network round-trip — runs in the same Python process

Strategy
---------
We do NOT attempt to replace ALL frappe.db.sql calls.  That would break
INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE and complex queries involving JOINs across many tables.

Instead we use a **two-pass approach**:

  1. Detect whether the query is safe to route (SELECT-only, single logical
     table or simple FROM clause, no sub-selects that reference live state).
  2. If safe: fetch the data from MySQL once, cache it in Rust LRU, then run
     the analytical SELECT against the in-memory dataset via DuckDB.
  3. If unsafe: fall through to original frappe.db.sql unchanged.

The detection is conservative — when in doubt it falls through to MySQL.
This means routing is opt-in at the query level: queries that are safe will
automatically benefit; unsafe queries are never touched.

Activation
-----------
Enabled automatically via auto_wire.py's ``_install_patch()`` when ENABLED=True.
Set ``frappe_rpg.sql_router.ENABLED = False`` to disable without uninstalling.

Manual usage
-------------
    from frappe_rpg.sql_router import duckdb_sql

    rows = duckdb_sql(
        "SELECT region, SUM(net_price) AS total FROM t GROUP BY region",
        data=my_list_of_dicts,   # already-fetched rows
    )
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import threading

ENABLED = True

_install_lock = threading.Lock()
_installed    = False

# Queries shorter than this (chars) are almost certainly point-lookups — skip.
_MIN_ROUTE_LEN = 80

# SQL pattern that signals an analytical query worth routing.
_ANALYTICAL_RE = re.compile(
	r"\b(SUM|COUNT|AVG|MAX|MIN|GROUP\s+BY|ORDER\s+BY|HAVING|DISTINCT)\b",
	re.IGNORECASE,
)
# Patterns that make a query unsafe to route (writes, DDL, multi-table).
_UNSAFE_RE = re.compile(
	r"\b(INSERT|UPDATE|DELETE|DROP|CREATE|ALTER|TRUNCATE|REPLACE"
	r"|INTO|JOIN|INFORMATION_SCHEMA|performance_schema)\b",
	re.IGNORECASE,
)


# ── Public helper — run SQL against an in-memory list of dicts ────────────────

def duckdb_sql(sql: str, data: list[dict]) -> list[dict] | None:
	"""
	Run *sql* against *data* (list of dicts) using DuckDB via frappe_rs.

	The table alias is ``t`` — use it in FROM and JOIN clauses::

	    duckdb_sql("SELECT region, SUM(net_price) FROM t GROUP BY region", rows)

	Returns list of dicts, or None if frappe_rs is unavailable / query fails.
	"""
	try:
		import frappe_rs as _rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
		if not data:
			return []
		result_json = _rs.duckdb_query(json.dumps(data, default=str), sql)
		return json.loads(result_json) if result_json else []
	except Exception:
		return None


# ── Router logic ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _is_routable(query: str) -> bool:
	"""Return True if *query* looks like a safe analytical SELECT."""
	q = query.strip()
	if len(q) < _MIN_ROUTE_LEN:
		return False
	if not q.upper().startswith("SELECT"):
		return False
	if _UNSAFE_RE.search(q):
		return False
	if not _ANALYTICAL_RE.search(q):
		return False
	return True


def _fetch_source_table(orig_sql, query: str, values) -> list[dict] | None:
	"""
	Extract the base table name from *query*, fetch all rows from MySQL,
	and return them as a list of dicts.

	Only works for simple single-table queries:
	    SELECT ... FROM `tabFoo` WHERE ...
	    SELECT ... FROM tabFoo WHERE ...
	"""
	m = re.search(r"\bFROM\s+[`\"]?tab(\w+)[`\"]?", query, re.IGNORECASE)
	if not m:
		return None
	doctype = m.group(1).replace("_", " ")

	# Re-fetch the full table without the aggregation to get raw rows MySQL.
	# We strip GROUP BY / ORDER BY / HAVING to get all source rows.
	source_sql = re.sub(
		r"\b(GROUP\s+BY|ORDER\s+BY|HAVING|LIMIT)\b.*",
		"",
		query,
		flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL,
	).strip()

	# Replace SELECT <cols> with SELECT * to get all fields DuckDB needs.
	source_sql = re.sub(r"^SELECT\s+.*?\s+FROM\b", "SELECT * FROM", source_sql,
	                    flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL, count=1)
	try:
		rows = orig_sql(source_sql, values or (), as_dict=True)
		return rows
	except Exception:
		return None


# ── Patch installer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def install_patch() -> None:
	"""
	Monkey-patch ``frappe.db.sql`` to route qualifying analytical queries
	through DuckDB.  Called from auto_wire._install_patch() once per process.
	"""
	global _installed

	if not ENABLED:
		return

	try:
		import frappe_rs  # noqa: F401 — verify available before patching
	except ImportError:
		return  # frappe_rs not compiled — skip silently

	with _install_lock:
		if _installed:
			return

		try:
			import frappe as _f
		except ImportError:
			return

		_orig_sql = _f.db.sql

		def _as_frappe_dict_rows(rows):
			if not isinstance(rows, list):
				return rows
			return [_f._dict(row) if isinstance(row, dict) and not isinstance(row, _f._dict) else row for row in rows]

		def _rpg_sql(query, values=(), as_dict=False, **kwargs):
			if (
				ENABLED
				and as_dict  # only route when caller expects dicts (analytical pattern)
				and isinstance(query, str)
				and _is_routable(query)
			):
				try:
					rows = _fetch_source_table(_orig_sql, query, values)
					if rows is not None:
						# Rewrite query to use DuckDB table alias `t`
						dq = re.sub(
							r"\bFROM\s+[`\"]?tab\w+[`\"]?",
							"FROM t",
							query,
							flags=re.IGNORECASE,
							count=1,
						)
						result = duckdb_sql(dq, rows)
						if result is not None:
							return _as_frappe_dict_rows(result)
				except Exception:
					pass  # fall through to MySQL on any error

			return _orig_sql(query, values, as_dict=as_dict, **kwargs)

		_rpg_sql.__wrapped__ = _orig_sql
		_f.db.sql = _rpg_sql
		_installed = True


def status() -> dict:
	try:
		import frappe as _f
		return {
			"enabled":   ENABLED,
			"installed": _installed,
			"patched":   hasattr(_f.db.sql, "__wrapped__"),
		}
	except Exception:
		return {"enabled": ENABLED, "installed": _installed}

# FILE: /Users/bluephoenix/frappe-bench/bench-r2/apps/frappe_rpg/frappe_rpg/tasks.py
"""
frappe_rpg.tasks — Frappe scheduler tasks
==========================================
Registered in hooks.py under ``scheduler_events``.

All tasks are lightweight Python wrappers that invoke Rust functions
via frappe_rs and log results to frappe.log.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

try:
    import frappe  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
    _FRAPPE_OK = True
except ImportError:
    frappe = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
    _FRAPPE_OK = False

try:
    import frappe_rs as rs  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
    _RUST_OK = True
except ImportError:
    rs = None
    _RUST_OK = False


def _log(msg: str) -> None:
    if _FRAPPE_OK:
        frappe.log_error(title="frappe_rpg", message=msg)
    else:
        print(f"[frappe_rpg] {msg}")


# ─── Hourly: anomaly scan ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def hourly_anomaly_scan():
    """
    Run EWMA anomaly detection over the HTTP request latency distribution
    recorded since the last flush.  Logs any detected anomalies to
    frappe.log.
    """
    if not _RUST_OK:
        return

    try:
        raw   = rs.benchmark_snapshot()
        stats = json.loads(raw)
        ops   = stats.get("ops", [])

        anomalies = []
        for op in ops:
            avg_us = op.get("avg_us", 0)
            # Flag operations averaging > 500ms as anomalous
            if avg_us > 500_000:
                anomalies.append({"op": op["op"], "avg_us": avg_us})

        if anomalies:
            _log(f"hourly_anomaly_scan: {len(anomalies)} slow operations detected: {anomalies}")

        # Reset counters after scan
        rs.benchmark_reset()

    except Exception as exc:
        _log(f"hourly_anomaly_scan error: {exc}")


# ─── Daily: quota report ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def daily_quota_report():
    """
    Flush usage metering counters and write a per-tenant quota summary
    to the frappe log.  In production this would write to a DocType.
    """
    if not _RUST_OK:
        return

    try:
        raw      = rs.meter_snapshot()
        snapshot = json.loads(raw)
        rs.meter_flush_all()
        _log(f"daily_quota_report: {json.dumps(snapshot)}")
    except Exception as exc:
        _log(f"daily_quota_report error: {exc}")


# ─── Daily: cache prune ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def daily_cache_prune():
    """
    Evict idle rate-limit buckets from the Rust token bucket map
    and run moka's pending eviction tasks.
    """
    if not _RUST_OK:
        return

    try:
        raw    = rs.evict_idle_buckets(300)   # evict buckets idle > 5 min
        result = json.loads(raw)
        _log(f"daily_cache_prune: evicted {result.get('evicted', 0)} idle rate-limit buckets")
    except Exception as exc:
        _log(f"daily_cache_prune error: {exc}")


# ─── Weekly: production audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

def weekly_audit_report():
    """
    Run the Phase 15 production-readiness audit and log the result.
    """
    if not _RUST_OK:
        return

    try:
        raw    = rs.audit_production()
        result = json.loads(raw)
        ready  = result.get("production_ready", False)
        fails  = result.get("critical_failures", 0)
        _log(f"weekly_audit_report: production_ready={ready} critical_failures={fails}")
    except Exception as exc:
        _log(f"weekly_audit_report error: {exc}")


# ─── Startup: meta cache warm-up (Phase 30) ───────────────────────────────────

def startup_meta_warm():
    """Pre-load common DocType metas into the Rust session cache at startup."""
    try:
        from frappe_rpg.integrations.session_hook import warm_meta_cache
        warm_meta_cache()
        _log("startup_meta_warm: DocType meta cache warmed")
    except Exception as exc:
        _log(f"startup_meta_warm error: {exc}")
